We employ a three-step process: census and geospatial data are averaged into intermediate village-cluster-scale tessellations to reduce noise and regularize administrative boundary changes; an autoencoder compresses high-dimensional National Sample Survey Office (NSSO) data into a low-dimensional latent representation; and a regression model maps upscaled census and geospatial data to this representation. This function is applied to fine-grained census data to generate high-resolution predictions, validated against ground-truth district-level NSSO indicators. Results confirm the methodology predicts socioeconomic indicators at fine scales with strong accuracy.

</details>


### [249] [Compact Latent Coordination for Autonomous Vehicles at Unsignalized Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21488)
*Gil Lifshits,Igal Bilik,Gilad Katz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MAPS，一种主-智能体原型计划系统，即分层深度强化学习架构，其中集中式主智能体生成紧凑的连续嵌入（称为原型计划）编码全局协调策略，分散式工作智能体将嵌入与局部观测结合执行车辆特定控制，在72种交叉口配置中实现无碰撞导航并显著降低平均行程时间，且表现出强泛化能力（3智能体训练零样本迁移至5智能体场景成功率94%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无信号交叉口的自主车辆协调是多智能体强化学习系统的关键挑战，现有方法在组合动作空间、依赖特权信息或刚性智能体设计方面存在局限性。

Method: 提出基于主-智能体原型计划系统（MAPS）的分层深度强化学习架构：集中式主智能体生成全局协调策略的紧凑连续嵌入（原型计划）；分散式工作智能体将嵌入与局部观测结合执行车辆控制，实现策略意图与战术执行解耦，支持各模块独立优化。

Result: 在72种HighwayEnv交叉口配置中，MAPS实现无碰撞导航，显著降低平均行程时间，优于现有基线。3智能体训练的系统在零样本迁移至5智能体场景时成功率94%。

Conclusion: 基于原型计划的分层学习为多车协调提供了有前景的框架。

Abstract: Coordinating autonomous vehicles at unsignalized intersections remains a critical challenge for multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems, which typically struggle with combinatorial action spaces, reliance on privileged information, or rigid agent designs. We propose Master-Agent Proto-plan System (MAPS), a hierarchical deep reinforcement learning (DRL) architecture in which a centralized Master agent generates a compact, continuous embedding, denoted as proto-plan, that encodes a global coordination strategy. Decentralized Worker agents integrate this embedding with local observations to execute vehicle-specific control, decoupling strategic intent from tactical execution and enabling independent optimization of each module.
  As a proof-of-concept evaluation of this coordination mechanism, we test MAPS across 72 intersection configurations in HighwayEnv. MAPS achieves collision-free navigation while significantly reducing average travel time, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. The learned proto-plans further exhibit robust generalization: a system trained with three agents achieves a 94% success rate when deployed zero-shot to five-agent scenarios, confirming that proto-plan-based hierarchical learning provides a promising framework for multi-vehicle coordination.

</details>


### [250] [Chronofy: A Temporal-Logical Decay Architecture for Information Validity in Time-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20560)
*Muntaser Syed,Marius Silaghi,Sheikh Abujar,Sharun Akter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chronofy 是一个三层的神经符号框架，通过将时间有效性直接嵌入表示、检索和推理层来解决 RAG 系统中的时间幻觉问题，利用 Matryoshka 嵌入中的时间子空间、可学习的指数衰减函数和信号时序逻辑鲁棒性函数来确保时间上过时的事实不会污染 LLM 输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 RAG 系统将所有检索到的事实视为同等有效，忽略了时间来源，导致时间幻觉——合理但已过时的事实污染了输出结果。例如，昨天的临床化验结果具有可操作性，而六个月前的相同结果只是噪音。

Method: 提出 Chronofy 框架，包含三层：Layer 1 在 Matryoshka 嵌入中保留专用时间子空间，使事实年龄在结构上不可移除；Layer 2 将可学习的指数衰减函数集成到基于图的检索中，衰减系数 β_j 基于贝叶斯决策理论近似为潜在过程均值回归率的两倍；Layer 3 应用信号时序逻辑（STL）鲁棒性函数评估检索知识的时间有效性，并强制执行可能性的最弱环节原则，用推理链中衰减最严重的证据约束输出置信度。

Result: 在时序知识图谱预测基准、TimE 时序问答基准以及领域特定的敏感性分析上进行了评估，结果表明显式的时间衰减建模提高了检索精度，减少了时间幻觉，并在时间上下文不足时实现了原则性的数据重新获取触发机制。

Conclusion: 将时间衰减显式嵌入 RAG 系统的各个层次可以有效缓解时间幻觉问题，提升检索精度，并支持基于时间充分性的自适应数据重新获取决策。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems retrieve and integrate external knowledge to ground large language model (LLM) outputs. However, current RAG architectures treat all retrieved facts as equally valid regardless of temporal provenance, leading to temporal hallucination, where plausible but obsolete facts corrupt the output. A clinical lab reading from yesterday is actionable; the same reading from six months ago is noise. We present Chronofy, a three-layer neuro-symbolic framework implementing the Temporal-Logical Decay Architecture (TLDA) that embeds temporal validity directly into the representation, retrieval, and reasoning layers of RAG systems. Layer 1 reserves a dedicated temporal subspace within Matryoshka embeddings to make fact age structurally irremovable from the representation. Layer 2 integrates learnable exponential decay functions into graph-based retrieval, where the decay coefficient $β_j$ is grounded in Bayesian decision theory as an approximation of twice the latent process mean-reversion rate. Layer 3 applies Signal Temporal Logic (STL) robustness functions to evaluate the temporal validity of retrieved knowledge, not LLM output confidence, and enforces the possibilistic weakest-link principle to bound output confidence by the most decayed evidence in the reasoning chain. We evaluate Chronofy on temporal knowledge graph forecasting benchmarks, the TimE temporal QA benchmark, and a domain-specific sensitivity analysis, demonstrating that explicit temporal decay modeling improves retrieval precision, reduces temporal hallucination, and enables principled data re-acquisition triggers when temporal context is insufficient.

</details>


### [251] [CT-Merging: Consensus Directions and Task-Level Scaling for LoRA Adapter Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20561)
*Keumseo Ryum,Joonhyuk Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CT-Merging方法，通过估计共识方向和任务级RMS系数缩放，解决LoRA适配器合并中SVD系数与任务更新不匹配的问题，在多任务合并基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SVD-based LoRA合并方法在构建共享方向后，直接继承原始任务的SVD系数，导致系数大小与任务实际更新诱导的系数存在显著差异，影响合并后适配器的多任务性能。

Method: CT-Merging：1) 从平均任务子空间投影器中估计共识方向；2) 在方向构建后分配任务级RMS系数缩放，而非使用原始SVD幅值；3) 利用任务SVD子空间的重复支持构建公共基。

Result: 在DC-Merge CLIP适配器基准上，CT-Merging的平均归一化准确率优于现有SOTA合并方法；在ViT-B/32和ViT-L/14的KnoTS训练检查点上分别比DC-Merge提升2.56和1.51个点。

Conclusion: CT-Merging通过LoRA感知的系数调整有效解决了SVD系数与任务更新的不匹配问题，是一种更优的多任务LoRA适配器合并方案。

Abstract: LoRA adapters provide an efficient way to specialize a pretrained model for many downstream tasks, but deploying one adapter per task requires adapter storage and task selection at inference time. Model merging addresses this issue by combining independently trained adapters into one multi-task adapter. Recent SVD-based LoRA merging methods mainly focus on constructing shared or task specific directions, while the coefficients assigned to the final directions are often directly from the original task SVD. On a fixed merged basis, inherited coefficients preserve component order with high rank correlation, yet their magnitudes differ substantially from the coefficients induced by the task updates. To address this mismatch, we propose CT-Merging, a LoRA-aware merging algorithm that estimates consensus directions from average task subspace projectors and assigns task-level RMS coefficient scales in the final update. CT-Merging uses repeated support across task SVD subspaces to construct the common basis, while reducing reliance on rank wise SVD magnitudes after direction construction. On the DC-Merge CLIP adapter benchmark, CT-Merging achieves superior average normalized accuracy compared to state-of-the-art merging methods and further improves over DC-Merge by 2.56 points on ViT-B/32 and 1.51 points on ViT-L/14 KnoTS-trained checkpoints.

</details>


### [252] [AI-Driven Surrogate Models for Predicting Electrode-Scale Discharge Behavior in Lithium-Ion Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20577)
*Mengda Xing,Jean-Marie Lagniez,Alejandro Franco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Swin3D Transformer的深度学习替代模型，结合高斯位置编码和时间编码模块，直接从体积数据预测锂离子电池电极尺度的时空放电动态，相比传统物理仿真计算成本降低数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于物理的仿真虽然对理解锂离子电池电极尺度放电行为至关重要，但计算成本过高，亟需高效的替代方法。

Method: 构建了Swin3D Transformer为基础的深度学习替代模型，创新点包括：(1) 高斯位置编码(GPE)适应电极微结构的复杂几何特征；(2) 时间编码模块捕捉非线性时间序列演化。

Result: 在电化学仿真数据集上，该方法的预测精度显著优于现有的点云基线模型，同时计算开销降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法为高通量电池设计与优化提供了可扩展且高效的框架，大幅降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Physics-based simulations are essential for understanding the electrode-scale discharge behavior of lithium-ion batteries (LIBs) but suffer from prohibitive computational costs. To address this, we introduce a novel deep learning surrogate pipeline based on the Swin3D Transformer to predict spatiotemporal discharge dynamics directly from volumetric data. Our approach integrates two key innovations: Gaussian Positional Encoding (GPE), which enhances spatial feature representation by adapting to the complex geometry of electrode microstructures, and a specialized Temporal Encoding module to capture non-linear timeseries evolution. Experimental validation on an Electrochemical Simulation (ES) dataset demonstrates that our pipeline significantly outperforms state-of-the-art point cloud baselines in prediction accuracy. Furthermore, the proposed method reduces the computational overhead by orders of magnitude, providing a scalable and efficient framework for high-throughput battery design and optimization.

</details>


### [253] [Fisher Widths: Local Learning Geometry and Anisotropic Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20578)
*Vu Khac Ky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过原始Fisher宽度和逆Fisher宽度研究了统计流形上的高斯宽度复杂性，证明了二者在学习和恢复中的互补统计角色，并建立了它们之间的尖锐关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对统计流形几何复杂性缺乏统一刻画，Fisher信息度量在不同任务（参数估计与稀疏恢复）中表现出不同的几何效应，亟需系统理解这两种宽度如何互补地影响统计学习与信号恢复。

Method: 定义原始Fisher宽度 $w_G(T)=w(G^{1/2}T)$ 和逆Fisher宽度 $w_{G^{-1}}(T)=w(G^{-1/2}T)$；在学习侧证明Fisher正则损失下 $w_G(H_r)/\sqrt n$ 在足够小的Fisher球上可达；在恢复侧利用逆Fisher宽度刻画各向异性高斯测量协方差，获得统计维数的双侧估计和支持敏感恢复界；最后推导出 $w_G(T)w_{G^{-1}}(T)\geq w(T)^2$ 的下界关系。

Result: Fisher宽度衡量局部参数波动在Fisher信息几何中的尺度；逆Fisher宽度捕捉依赖于Fisher谱中活动坐标位置的恢复几何；两者乘积在任意紧集上不小于欧几里得宽度平方，表明Fisher各向异性无法同时压缩两种几何下的复杂度。

Conclusion: 原始与逆Fisher宽度在统计流形上发挥着互补作用：一个主导学习复杂度，一个主导恢复复杂度，且二者满足不可同时减小的乘积下界关系，为理解Fisher几何中的复杂性权衡提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study Gaussian-width complexity on statistical manifolds through a pair of functionals: the primal Fisher width $w_G(T) = w(G^{1/2}T)$, induced by the Fisher metric, and the inverse-Fisher width $w_{G^{-1}}(T) = w(G^{-1/2}T)$, induced by the inverse Fisher metric. The two widths play complementary statistical roles.
  On the learning side, the Fisher width measures the size of local parameter fluctuations in the geometry induced by the Fisher information. For Fisher-regular losses, we prove that the scale \(w_G(H_r)/\sqrt n\) is attained on sufficiently small Fisher balls.
  On the recovery side, the inverse-Fisher width captures the effect of anisotropic Gaussian measurements whose covariance is determined by the inverse Fisher information. For sparse recovery, the resulting geometry depends not only on sparsity but also on the position of the active coordinates in the Fisher spectrum. We obtain a two-sided estimate for the corresponding statistical dimension, together with support-sensitive recovery estimates and a natural ordering of supports with different curvature profiles.
  Finally, we establish a sharp relation between the primal and inverse-Fisher widths. On any common compact coordinate set $T$, they satisfy \[ w_G(T)w_{G^{-1}}(T)\geq w(T)^2. \] Thus, Fisher anisotropy may transfer complexity from one geometry to the other, but cannot reduce both widths relative to the Euclidean scale.

</details>


### [254] [Bayesian uncertainty estimation improves clinical decision making in medical AI agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20582)
*Frederik Hauke,Patrick Wienholt,Christiane Kuhl,Dyke Ferber,Jakob Nikolas Kather,Sven Nebelung,Daniel Truhn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出蒙特卡洛dropout方法为胸部X光片多任务分类模型提供认知不确定性信号，该信号能追踪泛化能力并标记高置信度的错误预测。将不确定性加入点预测后，错误检测AUROC从0.74提升至0.77。在临床决策支持实验中，仅当以二值错误风险标志而非原始分数形式传达时，不确定性才能有效减少不可靠发现上的错误诊断（从8.5%降至2.7%）。认知不确定性包含超越点预测的决策相关信息，但其价值取决于传达方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分析模型通常缺乏可靠的置信度度量，限制了其在模糊或非典型病例中的应用。

Method: 在胸部X光片多任务分类器（8种胸部病变，137,593张训练图像）上应用蒙特卡洛dropout来估计认知不确定性；通过受控2x2因子实验比较不确定性以原始分数和二值错误风险标志两种方式传达给临床决策支持系统时的效果。

Result: 加入不确定性信号后错误检测AUROC从0.74提升至0.77（ΔAUROC +0.023，95% CI [+0.014, +0.033]）；以二值错误风险标志传达时，不可靠发现上的错误诊断率从8.5%降至2.7%。

Conclusion: 认知不确定性估计包含超越点预测的决策相关信息，但其对下游决策支持系统的价值取决于传达方式（二值标志优于原始分数）。

Abstract: Machine learning models for medical image analysis typically lack a reliable measure of confidence, limiting their use in ambiguous or atypical cases. Here we show that Monte Carlo dropout, applied to a multi-task chest-radiograph classifier (eight thoracic findings, 137,593 training images), provides an epistemic uncertainty signal that tracks generalisation across training-set scales and flags confident yet error-prone predictions. Adding this signal to the point prediction raised error-detection AUROC from 0.74 to 0.77 ($Δ$AUROC +0.023, 95% CI [+0.014, +0.033]). In a controlled 2x2 factorial experiment, a clinical-decision-support agent exploited this uncertainty only when it was delivered as a binary error-risk flag rather than as raw scores, cutting confident misdiagnoses on unreliable findings from 8.5% to 2.7%. Epistemic uncertainty estimation thus carries decision-relevant information beyond point predictions, but its value for downstream agents depends on how it is communicated.

</details>


### [255] [Exact ReLU realization of affine one-dimensional refinement iterates via residual memory and offset frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20586)
*Boldsaikhan Bolorkhuu,Tsogtgerel Gantumur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究向量值仿射细化算子，证明对于M≥3，每个有限仿射迭代W^nγ可通过O(n)深度的固定宽度ReLU网络精确实现。核心创新是残差记忆控制器，将不可逆残差动力学替换为单射斜积，实现残差状态的精确回放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究向量值仿射细化算子的ReLU网络实现，解决非可逆残差动力学导致的精确回放问题。

Method: 提出残差记忆控制器，通过单射斜积替换不可逆残差动力学；采用偏移帧对齐强制原子与残差接缝；利用互补循环读出恢复精确值。

Result: 对于M≥3，任意紧支撑连续分段线性强制项，有限仿射迭代W^nγ存在O(n)深度的固定宽度ReLU实现；对于M=2，在普通帧接缝分离条件下同样适用。

Conclusion: 该方法可扩展至有限维连续分段线性跨度内的强制项，并为开曲线、有限状态及Hilbert/Morton型递归构造提供了线性深度提升。

Abstract: We study vector-valued affine refinement operators of the form [
(Wγ)(t)=\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}} A_jγ(Mt-j)+B(t), ]
with finitely supported matrix mask and compactly supported continuous piecewise linear input and forcing data. Building on the homogeneous realization theorem for (B\equiv 0), we prove that, for (M\ge 3), every finite affine iterate (W^nγ) admits an exact fixed-width ReLU realization whose depth is (O(n)).
  The main new ingredient is a residual memory controller. It replaces the noninvertible residual dynamics by an injective skew-product and permits exact backward replay of the residual states required by a Horner-type evaluation of the affine forcing sum. Offset frames align the forcing atoms away from residual seams, allowing complementary loop readouts to recover their values exactly. The remaining branch-selection ambiguity occurs only where the accumulated affine state has already vanished.
  For (M\ge 3), the result applies to arbitrary compactly supported continuous piecewise linear forcing terms. For (M=2), the same construction applies to ordinary-frame seam-separated forcing. We also prove a stage-dependent extension for forcing terms in a fixed finite-dimensional continuous piecewise linear span and record the resulting linear-depth upgrade for open-curve, finite-state, and Hilbert- and Morton-type recursive constructions.

</details>


### [256] [Detecting Neural Network Failures through Spectral Analysis of Internal Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20590)
*Arunan J*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现神经网络误分类时内部激活存在频谱不稳定性（频谱漂移），并提出自检测神经网络（SDNN），通过监控各层频谱动态来检测错误，在CIFAR-10上AUROC达79.0%，远超置信度基线方法约25-30个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于输出置信度的错误检测方法效果有限，因为内部处理中存在的频谱不稳定性在最终输出层被掩盖。本文旨在利用这一内部频谱特征来改进神经网络错误检测能力。

Method: 首先形式化定义"频谱漂移"（频域中层间激活的距离），实证验证其与误分类的显著关联。然后提出SDNN框架，利用短时傅里叶变换、小波分解和统计矩提取多尺度频谱特征，通过轻量级检测器网络（5%参数开销）在课程学习范式下识别失败模式。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，SDNN取得79.0±25.3% AUROC（三颗种子），显著优于MaxSoftmax（50.5%）和Energy Score（52.9%），提升约25-30个百分点。消融实验显示小波分解和统计特征贡献一致，STFT作用不明确。

Conclusion: 内部激活的频谱分析为神经网络可靠性提供了有前景的方向，揭示了基于输出的方法无法获得的诊断信息。

Abstract: Neural network misclassifications exhibit characteristic spectral instability in internal activations that is invisible at the output layer. This phenomenon is identified and formalized as Spectral Drift -- the frequency-domain distance between consecutive layer activations -- with empirical validation showing that failures exhibit significantly higher drift than correct predictions (1.9% increase, p<0.001). This spectral signature emerges during internal processing but becomes masked in final outputs, explaining why confidence-based detection methods struggle.
  This work introduces Self-Detecting Neural Networks (SDNN), a framework that monitors spectral dynamics across network depth using Short-Time Fourier Transform, wavelet decomposition, and statistical moments to capture multi-scale spectral features. A lightweight detector network (5% parameter overhead) learns to identify failure-indicative patterns via curriculum learning on progressively challenging distributions: natural misclassifications, distribution shifts, and adversarial perturbations.
  Experiments on CIFAR-10 demonstrate that SDNN achieves 79.0 +/- 25.3% AUROC across three seeds, substantially outperforming confidence-based baselines including MaxSoftmax (50.5%) and Energy Score (52.9%) by approximately 25-30 percentage points. Ablation studies reveal that wavelet decomposition and statistical features make consistent contributions, while STFT's role remains unclear. This work establishes spectral analysis of internal activations as a promising direction for neural network reliability, revealing diagnostic information inaccessible to output-based approaches.

</details>


### [257] [STeMP: Spatio-Temporal Modelling Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20592)
*Jan Linnenbrink,Jakub Nowosad,Marvin Ludwig,Anna Frederike Jablotschkin,Fabian Schumacher,Teja Kattenborn,Hanna Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STeMP（时空建模协议），旨在为时空机器学习建模提供标准化报告框架和建模指导，填补了该领域缺乏统一协议的空白。该协议包含概述、模型和预测三个部分，并配有R包和网页应用，支持半自动化填写和常见陷阱预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空机器学习建模在环境研究中应用广泛，但模型对训练数据特征和方法选择高度敏感，而目前缺乏标准化的报告协议来确保模型的可信度、透明度和可比性。

Method: 提出STeMP协议，包含三个部分：（1）概述部分记录元数据；（2）模型部分详述预测变量、评估方法和软件；（3）预测部分描述预测相关要素。配套R包包含网页应用，支持手动或半自动化填写协议，并在遇到常见陷阱时返回预警信息。

Result: STeMP协议托管于GitHub，配有R包和网页应用，支持半自动化填写和预警功能，可通过社区贡献持续完善。

Conclusion: STeMP填补了时空机器学习建模领域标准化报告协议的空白，有助于提升模型的可信度、透明度和可比性，同时为建模人员和审稿人提供指导支持。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal machine-learning modelling is an important tool in environmental research. However, machine-learning models are highly sensitive to both the characteristics of the training data, such as its distribution, and methodological choices, including the cross-validation strategy. Each decision has impact and implications on the model itself as well as the estimation of the model quality and applicability for certain purposes. Taking into account the large role of machine-learning based maps of the environment in science and their transfer into practice, transparent reporting of spatio-temporal models, ideally using standardized model protocols, is essential to enable trust, transparency and comparability. However, such protocols are currently lacking for spatio-temporal modelling.
  We propose STeMP (Spatio-Temporal Modelling Protocol) to fill this gap by serving two purposes: standardized reporting to understand the model functioning as well as providing guidance during the modelling process by pointing at critical decisions and parameters. The protocol is structured in three sections: Overview, Model and Prediction. The Overview section contains metadata, while the Model and Prediction sections go into detail, describing predictors, evaluation and software, and further relevant elements of the modelling workflow.
  The protocol definition is hosted on GitHub and accompanied by an R-package (https://github.com/LOEK-RS/STeMP). The R-package contains a web application that can be used to fill the protocol either manually or in a semi-automated way from provided modelling objects. Warnings are returned from the protocol when common pitfalls are encountered, which may help authors as a guide through the modelling process but also support reviewers in the assessment of modelling studies. Via GitHub, incorporation of contributions and feedback from the community is encouraged.

</details>


### [258] [When Does Recurrence Become an Algorithm? Convergence Selection in Weight-Tied Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20594)
*Tong Zhang,Junhao Hu,Yun Peng,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了权重共享的循环Transformer如何实现算法，发现了计算预算定律、算法选择的架构先验、复杂度边界的位置以及机制的可迁移性等四个关键发现，并提出了新的分析工具——收敛时间缩放τ(n,i)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究权重共享的循环Transformer（即同一模块重复应用T次）何时能够真正实现一个算法，以及训练和测试过程中的计算资源分配如何影响算法实现。

Method: 在群词问题（group word problems）的受控种群上进行实验，使用权重共享的循环Transformer，分析训练循环数T_train和测试循环数对算法实现的影响，并引入收敛时间缩放τ(n,i)作为新的分析工具，通过损伤锥（damage cones）进行因果验证。

Result: (1) 发现了计算预算定律：自由训练建立线性计算前沿v ~ n_train/T_train (指数0.98±0.04, R²=0.99)，在T=n训练时恰好为1；(2) 架构先验而非表达能力决定算法选择：标准深度Transformer学习并行扫描，权重共享使其转向串行前沿；(3) NC1完全性不增加计算成本（A5完全泛化），而群阶数导致困难（S5的120×120算子导致联合学习死锁），操作符优先的课程学习可消除障碍；(4) 机制可迁移但不可强制：跨预算契约的暖启动可迁移算法并重新定价速度，而通过输入调度强制串行性在自由训练成功时失败。

Conclusion: 权重共享的循环Transformer在循环次数与问题规模匹配时能实现算法，计算预算与算法速度之间存在精确的线性关系；架构先验在算法选择中起决定性作用；标准分析工具（如固定点分析）会饱和失效，而提出的τ(n,i)标度律能在分布外泛化场景中有效预测模型行为。

Abstract: When does a weight-tied looped transformer -- one block applied T times -- implement an actual algorithm? We answer with four findings from controlled populations on group word problems. (1) The budget law: free training installs a linear computation frontier, a mechanism that solves v positions per loop, whose speed is priced by the training contract: v ~ n_train/T_train (exponent 0.98 +/- 0.04, R^2=0.99), exactly unity under T=n training. SGD selects a frontier matching the minimum the contract demands; granting more test-time loops than ever trained rescues late positions at fixed input length, yielding a principled halting rule T* = ceil(n / v-hat). (2) Architecture prior, not expressivity, picks the algorithm: standard-depth transformers learn parallel scans on this family; weight tying flips the selection to the serial frontier, even when positional addressing for a log-depth scan is supplied. At matched depth and parameters, untied models extrapolate worst and fail to learn A5 at all. (3) The walls are not where circuit complexity says: NC1-completeness costs nothing (A5 generalizes fully), while group order does (S5's 120x120 operator deadlocks joint learning) -- and an operator-first curriculum dissolves the wall in every seed. (4) Mechanisms are portable, not mandatable: warm-starting across budget contracts transfers the algorithm in every seed, re-pricing its speed, while imposing seriality through the input schedule fails where free training succeeds. These results are invisible to standard instruments, which provably saturate at the fixed points trained loops converge to. We introduce a head instrument, the convergence-time scaling tau(n,i), validate it causally via damage cones whose slope reproduces v, and show in-distribution head measurements predict out-of-distribution fate where tail metrics do not. Results replicate on the public easy-to-hard benchmark.

</details>


### [259] [Are Single-Token Sparse Autoencoder Features Causally Necessary? Layer-Depth and SAE-Family Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20596)
*Seonglae Cho,Zekun Wu,Kleyton Da Costa,Rishi Kalra,Ilham Wicaksono,Adriano Koshiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析3.9M个特征、6个模型和3个SAE家族，发现单token特征在解码器空间中聚集紧密（4.7倍），集中于浅层网络；消融实验在208个全层条件中有178个产生显著logit降低；跨家族因果差异大于同家族规模效应，不同SAE家族的特征因果稳定性取决于训练方法而非激活函数或规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征的因果角色在不同SAE家族间是否稳定，因为此前这一跨家族因果稳定性尚未被系统测试。

Method: 分析6个模型和3个SAE家族中的3.9M个特征，以单token特征作为诊断案例（因ground truth允许直接比较），在完整层深度使用零消融（zero-ablation）方法，对比跨家族与同家族内的因果差异，并控制激活函数进行比较。

Result: 单token特征在解码器空间中聚集紧密（4.7倍），集中于浅层网络。消融实验在208个全层条件中有178个产生显著logit降低。跨家族因果差异超过同家族规模效应：GemmaScope和BatchTopK特征因果稳定，而LlamaScope特征局部冗余。目标token排名在消融后96-98%恢复至2倍基线内。控制激活函数比较发现符号反转，表明训练方法是残差候选因素。

Conclusion: 跨家族的可解释性主张对训练方法敏感，而非仅仅受激活函数或规模影响。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoder (SAE) features are used to interpret and steer large language models, yet whether a feature's causal role is stable across SAE families remains untested. Single-token features that activate on one vocabulary item provide the diagnostic case where ground truth permits direct comparison. We analyze 3.9M features across six models and three SAE families using zero-ablation at full layer depth. Single-token features cluster 4.7x tighter in decoder space and concentrate in early layers (Layer 0 in GPT2-Small; L0-L4 in Gemma). Ablating them yields Benjamini-Hochberg-significant logit reductions in 178 of 208 full-layer conditions, with depth controlling whether damage cascades downstream or shapes the output directly. Cross-family causal differences exceed within-family scale effects: on the same base model, GemmaScope and BatchTopK features remain causally anchored, while LlamaScope features are locally redundant. The target token's rank recovers to within 2x baseline 96-98% of the time after the same ablation, and a controlled activation-function comparison reverses sign within the same model, leaving training recipe as the residual candidate. Cross-family interpretability claims are therefore sensitive to training methodology, not just activation function or scale.

</details>


### [260] [One Round Is All You Need: Analytic Federated Learning for Task-Heterogeneous Multi-Label Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20641)
*Afsaneh Mahanipour,Hana Khamfroush*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向任务异构场景的解析式联邦学习框架，用于多标签医学图像分类，仅需至多两轮通信即可完成训练，在ChestXray14数据集上比FedMLP提升高达18.44 BACC和13.24 AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习方法在任务异构场景下（各机构仅标注其专业领域的病理类别）面临两大问题：需要数百轮通信才能收敛，且缺失类别标签引入系统性假阴性偏差，缺乏修正机制。

Method: 用三个闭式操作替代迭代梯度优化：1) 平衡标签投影，通过归一化正负样本贡献消除类别不平衡偏差；2) 每类绝对聚合法则，独立从标注客户端上传的充分统计量组装最优岭回归分类器；3) 可选的解析式伪标签精炼轮次，通过置信度过滤的教师分类器向非标注客户端传播缺失类别知识。

Result: 在ChestXray14的四级渐进缺失类别配置下，所提方法一致优于FedMLP，最高提升18.44 BACC和13.24 AUC，且通信轮次大幅降低。

Conclusion: 提出的解析式联邦学习框架在任务异构的多标签医学图像分类中，以极低通信开销实现了显著更优的性能，有效解决了缺失标签偏差问题。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables multiple clinical institutions to collaboratively train a shared disease classifier without centralizing patient data. In practice, however, each institution annotates only the pathologies within its area of expertise, so the federation operates under task heterogeneity: each client holds labels for a strict subset of the target disease categories while the remaining classes are entirely unobserved at that site. Existing gradient-based FL methods fail under this setting because they require hundreds of communication rounds to converge and because missing class labels introduce systematic false-negative bias that the model cannot correct without a principled mechanism. We propose an analytic federated learning framework for multi-label medical image classification under task heterogeneity. The proposed method replaces iterative gradient optimization with three closed-form operations: a balanced label projection that neutralizes class-imbalance bias by normalizing positive and negative contributions to equal total mass; a per-class absolute aggregation law that independently assembles the optimal ridge-regression classifier for each disease category from the sufficient statistics uploaded by its annotating clients; and an optional analytic pseudo-label refinement round that propagates missing-class knowledge from a confidence-filtered teacher classifier to non-annotating clients. The entire procedure requires at most two communication rounds, irrespective of the degree of task heterogeneity or the number of participating clients. Experiments on ChestXray14 under four progressively severe missing-class configurations demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art federated multi-label method FedMLP by up to 18.44 BACC points and 13.24 AUC points, while reducing the communication.

</details>


### [261] [Scaling Interpretable Transformers with Parity Bottleneck Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20652)
*Andrew Mack,Kraig Yuheng Tou,Mark Henry,Zhengxun Wu,Lauren Greenspan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ParityTransformer架构，通过深度奇偶瓶颈(DPB)用无参数代数字典替代可学习过完备基，实现每层高效宽稀疏瓶颈，使模型内部表示天然可解释，性能优于或持平于后验稀疏自编码器(SAE)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型存在叠加现象，残差流中表示的特征远多于维度。后验SAE虽能恢复特征，但训练时每层过完备瓶颈在内存和计算上过于昂贵，导致按构造可解释的模型训练一直不切实际。

Method: 提出深度奇偶瓶颈(DPB)，用无参数代数字典替代可学习过完备基，提供确定性非相干性保证，消除内存需求；采用多级混合专家(MoE)方法高效强制稀疏性，通过硬件感知实现缩小激活稀疏与密集训练之间的成本差距。

Result: ParityTransformer在稀疏探测任务上表现至少与后验SAE相当，在特征吸收、引导有效性和细粒度因果干预方面表现更优。由于后续计算仅作用于通过稀疏瓶颈的特征，这些特征是模型前向传播的原生组成部分。

Conclusion: 这项工作朝着训练内部表示按构造可解释而非后验恢复的模型迈出了一步。

Abstract: Language models are thought to exhibit the phenomenon of superposition, representing many more features than dimensions in their residual streams. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are designed to recover such features post-hoc, but training models that are interpretable by construction has remained impractical, as a per-layer over-complete bottleneck is prohibitively expensive in both memory and compute. To overcome this issue, we introduce the ParityTransformer, a GPT-2-scale architecture whose intermediate representations are efficient and wide / sparse by design. At each layer, a Deep Parity Bottleneck (DPB) replaces a learned over-complete basis with a parameter-free algebraic dictionary, providing a deterministic incoherence guarantee and eliminating the memory requirements that have prevented per-layer interpretable bottlenecks at scale. A DPB is a hierarchically structured sparse bottleneck which efficiently enforces sparsity using a multi-level mixture-of-experts approach: a hardware-aware implementation that closes the cost gap between activation sparse and dense training to a manageable interpretability tax. Empirically, ParityTransformers perform at least as well as post-hoc SAEs on sparse probing tasks, while out-performing on measures of feature absorption, steering effectiveness, and fine-grained causal interventions. Because subsequent computation acts only on features that survive the sparse bottleneck, the ParityTransformer's features are native to the model's forwards pass by construction, addressing the question of whether SAEs probe features the model actually uses during computation. We see this as a step toward training models whose internal representations are interpretable by design rather than recovered post hoc.

</details>


### [262] [SalesLoop: Reinforcement Learning from Performance Feedback for Sales Lead Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20655)
*Chenyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SalesLoop是一个强化学习框架，通过闭环反馈机制解决CRM系统中线索排名的离线-在线性能差距，在160天生产A/B测试中实现显著的业务指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CRM系统中的线索排名面临离线模型高精度但生产环境中性能不佳的持续挑战，原因在于离线-在线指标不匹配、逐点-列表目标不一致以及时间分布漂移这三个根本性差距。

Method: 提出SalesLoop强化学习框架：(1) 性能感知奖励函数，编码转换结果并加权排名位置和转换速度；(2) Discriminative GRPO，一种将Group Relative Policy Optimization适配到判别式排序模型的列表级优化目标，在模型预测与真实业务结果之间建立闭环反馈。

Result: NDCG@K提升+7.9%，P@K提升+15.8%；在新能源汽车制造商的160天生产A/B测试中（覆盖1650万线索、280名销售专家、两个省级市场），累计提升+4.7%（p=0.047）和+8.7%（p=0.002）；排名主干模型Top-10%召回率达44.1%，高意向线索转化率为专家基线的2.3倍。

Conclusion: SalesLoop通过闭环强化学习框架有效弥合了CRM线索排名中离线与在线性能的差距，在实际生产环境中显著提升了线索转化率和排名质量，验证了业务驱动的排序优化范式的有效性。

Abstract: Lead ranking in Customer Relationship Management (CRM) systems faces a persistent challenge: models achieving high offline accuracy often underperform in production. We identify three fundamental gaps responsible for this disconnect: offline-online metric mismatch, pointwise-listwise objective misalignment, and temporal distribution drift. To address these gaps, we propose SalesLoop, a reinforcement learning framework that establishes a closed feedback loop between model predictions and real-world business outcomes. Our approach introduces (1) a performance-aware reward that encodes conversion outcomes weighted by ranking position and conversion velocity, and (2) Discriminative GRPO, a listwise optimization objective that adapts Group Relative Policy Optimization to discriminative ranking models.
  SalesLoop improves NDCG@K by +7.9\% and P@K by +15.8\% over the strongest static baseline. A 160-day production A/B test at a New Energy Vehicle manufacturer, spanning 16.5M leads and 280 sales specialists across two provincial markets, validates statistically significant cumulative lift of +4.7\% ($p=0.047$) and +8.7\% ($p=0.002$). In production, the ranking backbone achieves Top-10\% recall of 44.1\% and surfaces high-intent leads at $2.3\times$ the conversion rate of specialist baselines.

</details>


### [263] [Adaptive Multi-Horizon Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20656)
*Manoosh Samiei,Doina Precup,Paul Masset*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种多时间尺度自适应折扣因子方法，在无需手动调参的情况下，通过灵活选择和组合时间视野，使强化学习智能体能够适应不同任务结构和环境变化，在MiniGrid连续任务场景中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RL使用固定折扣因子施加单一指数衰减的时间视野，而生物智能体表现出灵活可变的时序折现能力，因此需要一种能自适应调整时间尺度的机制来应对复杂多变环境下的决策需求。

Method: 提出多时间视野（multi-horizon）方法，自适应地选择和组合多个时间尺度，无需人工调节折扣因子，从而在任务切换和环境配置变化时实现鲁棒适应。

Result: 在多个MiniGrid环境（包括三个连续变化任务的持续学习场景）中，该方法能够自动识别出有效的折扣因子，展现出良好的参数效率和适应性。

Conclusion: 自适应时序折现能够提升人工和类脑学习系统的参数效率与适应能力，尤其适用于持续学习场景。

Abstract: Effective decision-making in complex and changing environments requires balancing short-term and long-term consequences. In reinforcement learning (RL), this trade-off is typically controlled through a fixed discount factor, which imposes a single exponentially discounted temporal horizon. However, biological agents exhibit flexible and adaptive temporal discounting, suggesting that effective planning requires multiple timescales. Here, we propose a multi-horizon approach that adaptively selects and combines temporal horizons, enabling robust adaptation to changes in reward structure without manual discount-factor tuning. This flexibility makes the method particularly suitable for continual learning scenarios involving task switches and varying environmental configurations. Empirically, we demonstrate that our approach identifies effective discount factors across a range of MiniGrid environments, including continual settings composed of three sequentially changing tasks. These results suggest that adaptive temporal discounting can improve parameter efficiency and enhance adaptability in both artificial and biologically inspired learning systems.

</details>


### [264] [End-to-End Learning of Safe Optimal Feedback Control in High Dimensions with Control Barrier Function Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20674)
*Xingjian Li,Kelvin Kan,Deepanshu Verma,Krishna Kumar,Stanley Osher,Samy Wu Fung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合算子分裂与无雅可比反向传播（JFB）的方法，实现了包含控制屏障函数（CBF）安全滤波器的高维系统端到端训练，在保持硬安全约束的同时将可扩展维度从16提升至1200状态维度和400控制维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于CBF的安全强化学习方法由于嵌入二次规划安全滤波器的计算和微分瓶颈，仅适用于低维系统（通常最多16状态维度），无法扩展到高维多智能体系统。

Method: 将算子分裂（operator splitting）与雅可比自由反向传播（JFB）方法相结合，在端到端策略训练中实现可扩展的梯度计算，并利用非光滑分析理论为训练方法提供理论保证。

Result: 在高维多智能体非线性控制问题（状态维度1200、控制维度400）上验证了有效性，远超此前方法的能力范围（16维）。

Conclusion: 该方法通过算子分裂和JFB的结合有效克服了CBF安全滤波器的计算与微分瓶颈，实现了高维系统下硬安全约束的端到端学习。

Abstract: We consider the problem of learning high-dimensional semi-global feedback controllers under hard safety constraints enforced by control barrier functions (CBFs). Incorporating CBFs into end-to-end policy training requires embedding a quadratic-program-based safety filter as an optimization layer, but computational and differentiation bottlenecks have largely restricted prior approaches to low-dimensional systems, typically with at most 16 state dimensions. We address this limitation by combining operator splitting with the recently developed Jacobian-Free Backpropagation (JFB) method to enable scalable end-to-end training while preserving hard safety guarantees through the CBF safety filter. We justify this training methodology theoretically using nonsmooth analysis techniques and demonstrate its effectiveness on high-dimensional multi-agent nonlinear control problems with state and control dimensions up to 1200 and 400, respectively.

</details>


### [265] [Explanation-Based Runtime Verification for Trustworthy ML-driven Optical Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20675)
*Omran Ayoub,Carlos Natalino,Ali Al Housseini,Felix Foschum,Philipp Morger,Tiziano Leidi,David Hock,Paolo Monti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于解释的运行时验证方法，利用模型解释（如特征影响和物理一致性）在光网络控制环中评估机器学习决策的合理性，在光路传输质量分类场景中有效拦截错误决策同时保持高自动化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在光网络自动化中的部署日益增多，但错误决策直接影响服务质量、资源效率和网络稳定性，现有方法缺乏对个体决策在部署时的可靠性保障。

Method: 提出解释驱动的运行时验证方法，在控制环执行前评估模型解释的连贯性和物理基础一致性，对标记为不确定的决策进行延迟或拒绝。

Result: 在光路传输质量分类任务上，该方法能够拦截显著比例的错误决策，同时保持较高的自动化率。

Conclusion: 基于解释的运行时验证能有效提升光网络自动化中ML决策的可靠性，为可信AI在网络控制环中的安全部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) models are increasingly integrated into optical network automation frameworks to support tasks such as failure management, performance monitoring and resource allocation. In these environments, ML-driven predictions may be directly coupled with control-plane actions where incorrect decisions can immediately impact service quality, resource efficiency, and network stability. As automation levels increase, ensuring the reliability of individual decisions at deployment time becomes a critical requirement. Explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) techniques have emerged to improve transparency by highlighting the factors influencing ML predictions. In addition to identifying influential features, they provide insights into the underlying reasoning process of the model, revealing how different input variables contribute to the final outcome and how feature interactions shape the decision boundary. In this work, we introduce explanation-based runtime verification, an approach that exploits model explanations to assess the soundness of individual ML decisions before they are executed in the network control loop. The proposed approach evaluates explanation coherence and physics grounding consistency at runtime, enabling the system to defer or reject decisions flagged as uncertain. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on a representative use case of lightpath quality of transmission classification. Experimental results show that explanation-based verification can intercept a significant fraction of erroneous decisions while preserving high automation rate.

</details>


### [266] [Attribution Markets: A Fisher-Market Formulation for Fractional Credit Assignment Between Planned Tasks and Performed Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20694)
*Salavat Ishbulatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将个人与组织规划系统中“计划预算”与“实际日志”之间的脱节问题建模为准线性Fisher市场，通过熵正则化推广实现了噪声鲁棒的关联匹配，并提供了理论保证与实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统用独占的、全有或全无的链接桥接计划任务与实际执行动作，导致真正相关的未链接项被孤立，活跃目标被错误报告为停滞。需要一个更灵活、鲁棒的桥接机制。

Method: 将问题建模为准线性Fisher市场：计划任务作为预算约束的买家，执行动作作为可分割商品，融合文本/结构/时序信号的估值。引入卖方保留价和买方现金选项实现守恒性、硬预算上限和可证明的垃圾过滤。扩展出凹完成效用折扣，通过饱和阈值不动点（Brouwer）保证存在性和局部唯一性（对角占优条件）。进一步提出熵正则化泛化统一尖锐与平滑均衡，并给出噪声自适应规则。

Result: 标准市场算法的收敛理论在此不适用，通过饱和阈值不动点解决。去循环化多种子基准测试发现，市场的尖锐零熵均衡对亲和力噪声比熵正则化最优传输更敏感。熵正则化泛化有效统一了两者，噪声自适应规则进一步提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的熵正则化Fisher市场框架在理论上提供了守恒性、预算上限和垃圾过滤保证，在实践中通过噪声自适应规则提升了匹配的鲁棒性，与多触点归因、最优传输和在线Fisher市场算法相关。

Abstract: Personal and organizational planning systems maintain two records that drift apart: what was planned (a task's effort budget) and what was done (a logged action's duration and description). Existing systems bridge them with an exclusive, all-or-nothing link that strands genuinely related but unlinked effort and reports false stalls on active goals. We formulate the bridge as a quasi-linear Fisher market: planned tasks are budget-constrained buyers, performed actions are divisible goods, and a fused text/structural/temporal signal sets each buyer's valuation. Two market instruments - a seller reserve price and a buyer cash option - yield conservation, a hard budget cap, and a provable junk filter as theorems. We extend the market with a concave completion utility discounting progress as a task nears its plan; standard convergence theory for the market's algorithm does not transfer here, resolved by a satiation-threshold fixed point with existence (Brouwer) and local uniqueness under an explicit diagonal-dominance condition, validated empirically on random and adversarial instances. A de-circularized, multi-seed benchmark - observed affinity corrupted independently of the scored ground truth - surfaces a genuine weak spot: the market's sharp, zero-entropy equilibrium is more sensitive to affinity noise than entropy-regularized optimal transport's permanently smoothed one. We resolve this with a one-parameter entropy-regularized generalization unifying the two, plus a noise-adaptive rule for its regularization strength. We report full reproducibility parameters, discuss limitations candidly, and relate the result to multi-touch attribution, optimal transport, and online Fisher-market algorithms.

</details>


### [267] [CEDAR: Causal Edge Discovery for Autoregressive Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20696)
*Mohammad Fesanghary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CEDAR是一种基于约束的滞后因果边发现方法，适用于稀疏自回归时间序列。它通过AR(1)残差化距离相关筛选候选滞后，执行两次条件独立性测试，最多接受每对变量一个滞后，并利用稳定MCI剪枝去除间接边，支持趋势非平稳性调整。在稀疏场景下，筛选后仅需O(d²)次CI测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏自回归时间序列中，现有因果发现方法在数据稀缺、变量具有滞后1自动力学时效率或可解释性不足，需要一种在有限样本下高效、可解释的滞后边发现方法。

Method: (1) 使用AR(1)残差化、U中心距离相关筛选跨变量滞后候选；(2) 对每个显著候选执行两次针对性条件独立性测试，每对有序变量最多接受一个滞后；(3) 采用稳定MCI剪枝去除间接边；(4) 可选确定性C节点调整趋势类非平稳性。

Result: 在稀疏场景下（少量滞后通过筛选），CEDAR在筛选后仅需O(d²)次CI测试，同时保持边级可解释性。在数据稀缺且变量具有滞后1自动力学时最有效。

Conclusion: CEDAR适用于数据稀缺、滞后1自动力学主导的稀疏时间序列场景；当时间长度T增大或存在高阶自回归/多滞后效应时，具有更丰富条件集的替代方法更具优势。

Abstract: We propose CEDAR (Causal Edge Discovery for Autoregressive Processes), a constraint-based method for lagged causal edge discovery in sparse autoregressive time series. CEDAR screens candidate cross-variable lags using AR(1)-residualized, U-centered distance correlation, then applies two targeted conditional-independence tests per significant cross-variable lag candidate and accepts at most one lag per ordered pair. A stable MCI pruning step removes indirect edges, and optional deterministic C-nodes adjust for specified trend-like nonstationarity. In sparse regimes where few lags survive screening, CEDAR requires $O(d^2)$ CI tests after screening while retaining edge-level interpretability. CEDAR is most effective when data are scarce and variables exhibit lag-1 self-dynamics; methods with richer conditioning sets become preferable as $T$ grows or when higher-order autoregressive or simultaneous multi-lag effects are common.

</details>


### [268] [Perspective Latents as an Architectural Condition for Causal Emergence in Active Inference Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20708)
*Hongju Pae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在主动推理智能体中，$Φ_r$（集成信息分解）主要集中在慢速全局潜变量$g$上，其总体大小主要由架构决定且随训练减少。学习的实质效应只有在原子组成层面才可见：解耦从负变为正，并在环境变化下保持对环境的鲁棒性，而向下因果关系则承载了环境依赖的调整。这表明$Φ_r$不能作为学习整合的直接指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习智能体中，$Φ_r$随训练增长并追踪奖励改善，但对于主动推理（无奖励的预测性组织），$Φ_r$的行为尚不清楚。本文旨在探究主动推理智能体中$Φ_r$的分布特性及其与学习的关系。

Method: 设计了一个主动推理智能体，其架构分离快速感知潜变量$z$和慢速全局潜变量$g$，其中$g$由预测误差驱动且与策略梯度结构解耦。在无奖励的环境切换协议中测试$Φ_r$的行为。

Result: $Φ_r$集中在$g$中；其总体大小主要受架构决定且随训练减少。学习的实质效应在原子组成层面显现：解耦从负变为正且变得环境不变，向下因果关系承载环境依赖的调整。

Conclusion: $g$是$Φ_r$相关时间组织的架构位点；标量$Φ_r$不能作为学习整合的直接指标，需要原子层面的分析才能揭示学习效应。

Abstract: A recent line of work measures causal emergence in reinforcement learning agents through Integrated Information Decomposition, reporting that $Φ_r$ grows with training and tracks reward improvement. For active inference, this raises the question of how reward-free predictive organization relates to such information-theoretic signatures. I test this within an active inference agent whose architecture separates a fast perception latent $z$ from a slow global latent $g$, where $g$ is driven by prediction error and structurally decoupled from policy gradients. In a reward-free environmental regime-switching protocol, $Φ_r$ concentrates in $g$; its aggregate magnitude is largely architectural and decreases with training. The substantive effect of learning becomes legible only at the atom-compositional level: decoupling flips sign from negative to positive and becomes regime-invariant under environmental change, while downward causation carries the regime-dependent adjustment. These results identify $g$ as the architectural locus of $Φ_r$-relevant temporal organization in an active inference agent, and argue against reading scalar $Φ_r$ as a direct index of learned integration.

</details>


### [269] [LLMs Get Lost in Evolving User Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20734)
*Jihoon Tack,Philippe Laban,Jennifer Neville*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一个将静态单轮任务转化为动态多轮对话的框架，模拟用户意图的逐步揭示、修正和转变，发现当前LLM在静态设置中的强性能无法迁移到意图动态变化的场景，暴露出模型在追踪和响应演化中用户意图方面的根本性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM被越来越多地部署为协作代理，需要在与用户的迭代交互中理解动态演化的意图，但现有评估和训练仍主要基于单轮、完全指定的静态设定，缺乏对模型在会话中追踪和响应用户意图演化能力的系统性研究。

Method: 提出一个通用框架，将静态单轮任务转化为动态多轮对话，在保持原始评估协议的前提下，模拟用户意图在轮次间的增量揭示、修订和重定向，使现有基准可复用作受控测试平台。

Result: 在多个任务上观察到一致现象：强静态性能无法迁移到意图演化设定下，所有模型系列均出现显著的性能下降。

Conclusion: 当前LLM未能忠实地追踪和响应用户的演化中意图，这一能力对未来的协作代理至关重要，却在静态评估中完全不可见，指向了现有评估范式的根本性缺口。

Abstract: As LLMs become more capable, they are increasingly deployed as collaborative agents, taking on user-delegated tasks through iterative interaction. Yet genuine interaction is inherently dynamic: users rarely specify their intent upfront, instead disclosing, revising, and reshaping it as the conversation unfolds. Despite this, LLMs are still predominantly evaluated or trained in single-turn, fully-specified settings, leaving open a fundamental question: how well do LLMs track and act on user intent as it evolves over the course of a conversation? To study this, we introduce a framework that transforms static, single-turn tasks into dynamic multi-turn conversations in which the user's intent evolves across turns--incrementally revealed, revised, and at times redirected mid-conversation--while preserving each task's original evaluation protocol, enabling existing benchmarks to be reused as controlled testbeds without new annotation. Across multiple tasks, we surface a consistent phenomenon: strong static-setting performance does not transfer to the evolving-intent setting, with substantial drops across model families. Our findings point to a fundamental gap: today's LLMs do not yet faithfully track and act on the user's evolving intent, a capability invisible to static evaluation yet critical for future collaborative agents.

</details>


### [270] [Cardinality-Decomposed Loss: Matching Training Objectives to Relation Structure in Heterogeneous Recommendation Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20737)
*Parul Maheshwari,Amulya Paruchuri,Yiqing Zou,Alireza Sahami Shirazi,Farhad Farahani,Prakhar Mehrotra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基数分解损失（CDL），结合交叉熵和BPR损失，解决异质二分图中不同基数关系（如一对多 vs 一对一）导致的属性嵌入坍塌问题，在不影响推荐排序指标的前提下提升属性嵌入的可区分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统对异质二分图的所有边使用统一的BPR损失，导致一对一的属性嵌入退化为近随机几何（坍塌），而这种失效在标准排序指标（如NDCG）上不可见，但会污染用户节点嵌入，损害下游任务（个性化、分群等）。

Method: 提出Cardinality-Decomposed Loss (CDL)，将交叉熵（CE）和BPR损失结合，分别优化不同基数关系（一对一用CE，一对多用BPR），并通过λ参数调节两者权衡；同时揭示CE与BPR在共享编码器参数空间中的竞争关系。

Result: 在五个数据集（MovieLens-1M、Last.fm-360K、PayPal Audience Factory、BookCrossing、Yelp）上，CDL一致提升属性嵌入的可区分性；当属性蕴含偏好信号时，排序指标（NDCG）也提升；通过λ扫描发现数据集行为由语义对齐和拓扑泄漏两个图属性决定。

Conclusion: 单一BPR损失在异质图中会导致属性嵌入坍塌，CDL通过基数分解有效缓解该问题，属性嵌入质量的提升可服务于下游任务，且通过λ参数可灵活适应不同图结构特性。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks trained on heterogenous bipartite graphs form a common basis in recommendation systems. These graphs often express relations that vary in cardinality, for example, user-item preferences are one-to-many and user-attribute features are one-to-one. Traditionally, a unique loss function is applied for all of the network components which is often Bayesian Personalized Ranking (BPR). While BPR works well for the recommendation task, we find that it causes attribute embeddings to collapse to near-random geometry -- a silent failure that leaves standard ranking metrics largely unaffected and therefore invisible to conventional evaluation. This in turn pollutes user node embeddings, which are shaped by both edge types simultaneously, hurting downstream tasks like personalization, segmentation, etc. Here we propose a Cardinality-Decomposed Loss (CDL) that combines both Cross Entropy (CE) and BPR to enable the model to collectively optimize for relations across cardinalities. We confirm this CE-BPR conflict by showing the two losses compete in the shared encoder's parameter space. We evaluate CDL on five datasets spanning two structural configurations -- one-to-one attributes on user nodes (MovieLens-1M, Last.fm-360K, PayPal Audience Factory, BookCrossing) and on item nodes (Yelp) -- and find that CDL consistently improves discriminability in attribute embeddings. We also show that ranking (NDCG) improves when attributes carry meaningful preference signal, but conflicts with it when the correlation is weak. We use a lambda parameter to navigate this trade-off, and a lambda-sweep reveals that dataset behavior is governed by two graph properties -- semantic alignment and topology leakage. Semantic alignment measures whether the attribute predicts preferences, while topology leakage measures whether the graph's connectivity already encodes it.

</details>


### [271] [Adaptive Confidence-weighted Expansion for Trustworthy Multi-Omics Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20742)
*Mohammad Raahemi,Ali Sekhavati,Alireza Maleki,Hamid Nasiri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ACE框架通过生成新互补模态和双层置信机制，动态评估数据质量并输出可信置信度，在多组学数据集上显著优于现有方法，提升了多模态融合在安全关键场景中的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态融合方法缺乏对噪声/非信息数据流的鲁棒机制，无法动态评估数据质量并提供可信的置信度分数，限制了其在安全关键型医疗场景中的部署。

Method: (1) 利用模态内相关性生成新的互补模态以增强多模态空间；(2) 双层置信机制：融合前根据可靠性自适应重加权各模态，融合后估计全局信任分数。

Result: 在BRCA、KIPAN、LGG、ROSMAP四个多组学数据集上，ACE在分类性能和置信度校准两方面均显著优于现有最优算法。

Conclusion: ACE提供了一种更稳定鲁棒的数据融合方法，促进了多模态学习在高风险问题中的应用。

Abstract: Multimodal learning is a robust approach to improve predictive performance in applications such as medical prognosis. However, the clinical applicability of models that use multimodal learning is hampered by their poor performance under noisy or uninformative data streams. Present fusion approaches often lack robust mechanisms for the dynamic assessment of data quality and for the provision of a trustable confidence score on the final prediction. This dissuades their deployment in safety-critical settings. To address these limitations, we introduce Adaptive Confidence-weighted Expansion (ACE), a novel framework to enhance the trustworthiness of multimodal fusion models. ACE first enhances the multimodal space by generating new, complementary modalities from intra-modality correlations. It then employs a dual-level confidence mechanism that (1) adaptively reweighs all modalities by their reliability before fusion and (2) estimates a global trust score over the fused, final decision. To evaluate ACE, we used four challenging multi-omics datasets (BRCA, KIPAN, LGG, and ROSMAP). ACE significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art algorithms in both classification performance and confidence calibration. Our framework provides a more stable and robust data fusion method that facilitates the use of multimodal learning in addressing high-stakes problems.

</details>


### [272] [GaugeQuant: Online Learning of Quantization-Optimal Bases from LLM Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20757)
*Miguel P. Bento,João Seabra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GaugeQuant是一种利用Transformer内部连续对称性，通过在训练中引入LogSumExp项来打破对称性、选择最小化激活异常基的量化方法，在LLaMA-2 7B模型上显著降低了W4A4和W4A16量化下的困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型存在内部连续对称性，使得不同量化基下输出不变但量化效果不同，现有方法未能在训练中主动选择最优基以最小化激活异常值。

Method: 在训练损失中引入LogSumExp项来打破对称性，从而选择能最小化激活异常值的基；使用stop-gradient算子确保仅旋转矩阵被更新，语言建模目标函数完全不受影响。

Result: 在LLaMA-2 7B模型上，W4A4量化（组大小128）下困惑度从8.22降至6.73；W4A16量化下困惑度从11.16降至5.45。

Conclusion: GaugeQuant无需特定校准数据、无需量化模拟，且增加的计算开销可忽略不计，在量化效果上可与需要冻结模型和校准数据集的后训练方法相竞争。

Abstract: Transformers are known to have internal continuous symmetries that leave outputs invariant, while modifying quantization. GaugeQuant leverages this in-training by introducing a LogSumExp term to the loss that breaks the symmetries, thus selecting a basis that minimizes activation outliers. A stop-gradient operator ensures that only rotation matrices are updated, yielding the language modeling objective completely unaltered. Our requires no specific calibration data, no quantization simulation, and adds negligible training overhead. With the LLaMA-2 7B model under W4A4 quantization with group size 128, perplexity drops from 8.22 to 6.73, competing with post-training methods that require frozen models and calibration datasets. Under W4A16, perplexity drops from 11.16 to 5.45. Code is available at https://github.com/MPedraBento/gauge-quant.

</details>


### [273] [Memory-Computation Tradeoffs in Semi Amortized Parametric Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20769)
*Shijie Pan,Agustin Castellano,Zeyu Shen,Enrique Mallada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定在线计算预算下，离线信息（存储的求解实例）需要多少才能保证决策系统的精度。针对光滑凸参数优化问题，建立了匹配的上下界，揭示了记忆-计算-精度之间的权衡，并识别出加速的“记忆成本”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习驱动的决策系统常通过离线数据降低在线计算成本，但缺乏关于离线信息量、在线计算预算与最终精度之间关系的通用理论理解。本文旨在量化这三者之间的基本权衡。

Method: 通过摊销参数优化框架进行分析：离线阶段存储有限数量的已求解问题实例；在线阶段检索热启动并执行K步投影梯度下降。对于强凸目标和平滑凸目标分别建立记忆需求的理论上下界，识别出两个关键驱动量：在线优化器的收敛速率和解映射对问题参数的Lipschitz敏感度。

Result: 对μ-强凸目标，建立了保证ε精度所需记忆的匹配上下界；对满足β-增长条件（β>2）的凸目标，获得了近乎匹配的界，并发现K超过某个阈值后额外记忆不再带来收益。同时量化了加速的记忆成本，实验证实了理论预测。

Conclusion: 建立了离线记忆、在线计算与精度之间的理论权衡关系，为设计学习驱动的决策系统提供了指导。识别出加速需要付出记忆代价，且存在收益递减的相变点。

Abstract: Learning-enabled decision systems often use offline data or computation to reduce online compute cost. Despite the empirical success of such approaches, there is limited general understanding of how much offline information is needed to achieve a desired accuracy under a fixed online computation budget. We study this question through the lens of amortized parametric optimization: an offline phase stores a finite memory of solved problem instances, and an online phase produces a solution to a new instance by retrieving a warm start and applying $K$ steps of projected gradient descent. We analyze this setup for smooth convex parametric optimization over a compact domain, using a nonparametric predictor built from the stored offline solutions. For $μ$-strongly convex objectives, we establish matching upper and lower bounds on the memory required to guarantee $\varepsilon$-accuracy under a fixed online iteration budget $K$. For convex objectives satisfying a $β$-growth condition ($β>2$), we obtain near-matching bounds and identify a phase transition in $K$ beyond which additional memory provides no benefit. We further provide a general proof framework that (i) explicitly quantifies the memory cost of acceleration---how much offline memory is required to achieve a prescribed speedup over the unaided online optimizer---and (ii) identifies two key quantities driving this cost: the convergence rate of the online optimizer and the Lipschitz sensitivity of the solution map to the problem parameter. Experiments on parameterized ridge regression confirm the predicted memory--computation--accuracy tradeoffs.

</details>


### [274] [Toward Mechanistic Interpretability of an AI Foundation Model Fine-Tuned for Atmospheric Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20778)
*Jason Y. Hu,Ivan Higuera-Mendieta,Patrick Obin Sturm,Makoto M. Kelp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了微调后的天气基础模型（Aurora）在大气化学预测中学到了什么。通过受控化学扰动、内部表征分析和稀疏自编码器，发现该模型能捕捉一阶臭氧响应，但未强制执行过程模型中的化学约束，会产生化学不一致的组合，且内部表征主要围绕预训练时的气象学组织，缺乏化学特异性结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天气基础模型在空气污染预测中表现优异，但其高预测技能并不揭示模型是否学到了物理/化学机制还是仅利用训练数据中的统计规律。理解模型内部机制对于将其用于环境政策决策至关重要。

Method: 对Microsoft的Aurora模型施加受控化学扰动，测试其是否符合已知的光化学关系；检查生成这些预测的内部表征；使用稀疏自编码器识别因果控制化学预测的内部组件。

Result: Aurora能捕捉臭氧对活性氮的一阶响应，但未强制执行过程模型中的化学约束；产生化学不一致的组合；将局地排放特征（如野火羽流）向背景松弛；内部表征主要围绕气象学组织，缺乏化学特异性结构；稀疏自编码器识别的内部组件能因果控制化学预测，但未清晰映射到单个大气过程。

Conclusion: 为测试AI预测系统是否从再分析数据中学习大气化学提供了框架；主张成分预测不应仅以基准技能评判，还应考察其内部机制。

Abstract: Weather forecasting foundation models (FMs) are increasingly fine-tuned to predict air quality, offering fast global pollution forecasts at lower computational cost than conventional chemical transport models. These FMs are typically trained on reanalysis data and generate forecasts through autoregressive rollout. They do not explicitly represent governing physical or chemical processes. Therefore, high forecast skill does not reveal whether a model has learned physical mechanisms or exploits statistical regularities in its training data. Here, we present the first study of what a FM fine-tuned for atmospheric chemistry has learned by examining Microsoft's Aurora model. We impose controlled chemical perturbations on its forecasts and test them against known photochemical relationships. We then examine the internal representations that generate these forecasts. We find that Aurora captures a first-order ozone response to reactive nitrogen but does not enforce the chemical constraints that a process-based model encodes. It generates chemically inconsistent combinations of related species and relaxes localized emission features such as wildfire plumes toward background. Internally, its representations remain largely organized around the meteorology inherited during pretraining, with little structure specific to chemistry. Using sparse autoencoders, we identify internal components that causally control the chemical forecast but do not map cleanly onto individual atmospheric processes. This work provides a framework for testing whether AI forecasting systems learn atmospheric chemistry from reanalysis data. As these models are increasingly positioned to inform environmental policy decisions, we argue that composition forecasts should also be judged by their internal mechanisms rather than by benchmark skill alone.

</details>


### [275] [Synthetic minority data is redundant or invalid: a data-dependent validity theory and a de-biased test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20787)
*Ahmad B. Hassanat,Ahmad S. Tarawneh,Ghada A. Altarawneh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对类不平衡学习中合成少数类样本的经典验证方法进行了去偏处理，发现该验证方法存在系统性偏差（在96-99%的情况下低估了无效性）。作者提出了一致估计量，并证明有效性是数据属性而非方法属性：类别重叠设置了无效性下限，使得过采样在类别可分时冗余、在重叠时无效。对91种方法、3个分类器的评估表明，没有任何方法能同时满足有效性和信息增益两个标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类不平衡学习中，合成少数类样本的标准验证方法（将合成点与生成它们的数据本身进行对比）存在根本性偏差，无法真正评估合成数据的有效性。作者希望纠偏这一验证方法，并重新审视合成数据在类不平衡学习中的实际价值。

Method: (1) 将有效性定义为总体量——合成点真正属于少数类的概率，并提出基于 withheld 真实数据的一致估计量进行纠偏；(2) 理论证明类别重叠构成了合成数据无效性的下限；(3) 对91种过采样方法、3种分类器在医学和金融数据集上进行大规模实证评估。

Result: 经典检验在96-99%的方法-不平衡率组合中低估了真实无效性；所有91种方法均未能同时通过有效性和信息增益两个标准：相对于最优平凡基线的改进中位数低于0.01 F1，且多数方法损害了校准性能。

Conclusion: 合成少数类数据必须在使用时证明其有效性和信息增益，举证责任应从默认接受转向必须验证；过采样在类别重叠时本质上无效，在类别可分时则冗余。

Abstract: For two decades, the standard remedy for class-imbalanced learning has been to fabricate synthetic minority examples, and the standard evidence of their validity has been a check that cannot fail: synthetic points are scored against the very data that generated them. We de-bias the check. Validity becomes a population quantity -- the probability that a synthetic point truly belongs to the minority class -- with a consistent estimator that scores synthetic points against withheld real data. Where held-out ground truth is available, the classical test underestimates true invalidity in 96-99% of method-by-imbalance-ratio cells, while the de-biased estimator tracks it closely. We prove validity is a property of the data, not the method: class overlap sets an invalidity floor no faithful generator escapes, making oversampling redundant where classes separate and invalid where they overlap. Across 91 methods, three classifiers, and datasets spanning medicine and finance -- including a generator engineered to pass the classical check -- none clears both bars: gains over the best trivial baseline are noise-thin (median below 0.01 F1, a decision threshold's reach), and most damage calibration. We release the audit as a pip-installable test and flip the burden of proof: synthetic minority data must now demonstrate, on the data at hand, both validity and information gain.

</details>


### [276] [Memoir: Should a Model Write to Its Memory While It Thinks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20792)
*Jaber Jaber,Osama Jaber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Memoir结合了每样本快速记忆、共享慢参数、可变深度潜在循环和未来潜在能量目标。实验发现，在过程性关联回忆任务中，读写耦合臂的召回率(0.5203)低于只读对照臂(0.6557)，但经过960步训练后两者均达到1.0000，因此差异仅限于学习速度而非最终能力。未观察到记忆重写破坏能量信号的预测失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试Memoir中最具风险的设计：每次推理迭代既写入又读取快速记忆层的耦合机制是否有效。

Method: 设计配对实验，对比读写耦合的推理臂与仅读取不写入的对照臂，在过程性关联回忆任务上测试，控制参数、计算量、数据、优化器、调度和随机种子等变量。

Result: 240步训练后耦合臂召回率0.5203，只读臂0.6557，差异0.1354（配对t=3.23，10/12种子胜出）。960步后两者均达1.0000。未观察到记忆重写破坏能量信号的预测失败，能量裕度增长并保持。内核重构将delta规则前向时间从0.907ms降至0.351ms。

Conclusion: 读写耦合机制带来的是学习速度惩罚而非能力限制，且能量信号未如预期般被破坏；但长期控制在饱和任务上受限，非饱和任务的收敛性有待测量。

Abstract: Memoir combines per-sample fast memory, shared slow parameters, variable-depth latent recurrence, and a future-latent energy objective. We test its riskiest coupling: each pondering iteration may rewrite the fast tier that the same iteration reads. On procedural associative recall with key interference, we compare a coupled arm against an otherwise identical read-only pondering arm. Both arms contain 81,738 parameters, including 76,362 trainable parameters, and use matched declared forward multiply-accumulate counts, data, optimizer, schedule, and seeds. After 240 training steps across 12 seeds, coupled recall is 0.5203 with a 95 percent interval of [0.4522, 0.5883], while read-only recall is 0.6557 with [0.5953, 0.7160]. The arms are paired per seed, and the read-only lead of 0.1354 gives a paired t of 3.23 on 11 degrees of freedom with a 95 percent interval of [0.0431, 0.2277] on the difference, winning on 10 of 12 seeds. After 960 steps across 8 seeds, both arms reach 1.0000, so the measured effect is a learning-speed penalty at a fixed budget, not a demonstrated capability penalty. That longer control is ceiling limited, leaving convergence on a non-saturating task unmeasured. A predicted failure in which memory rewriting corrupts the energy signal did not occur: the energy margin grew and held. Kernel restructuring also reduced delta-rule forward time from 0.907 ms to 0.351 ms on the stated device. Code and evidence are available at https://github.com/RightNow-AI/Memoir

</details>


### [277] [External Clustering Validation by the Homogeneity-Parsimony Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20799)
*Andreas Tiffeau-Mayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于信息瓶颈原理的聚类同质性和简约性归一化评分，用于量化聚类结果与已知类标签之间的信息权衡，并通过数学证明和实验展示了其在聚类细化下具有单调性、统一了多种评估标准，并可用于特征选择和算法比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的标量指标在评估聚类与已知类的匹配程度时，会模糊一个基本权衡：聚类应当对类标签具有信息性，同时避免不必要的碎片化。现有指标无法清晰量化这一权衡。

Method: 基于信息瓶颈原理，但修改为不奖励有损压缩；定义了同质性和简约性的归一化评分；从香农熵扩展到集合匹配和基于对的设置；通过数学证明和实例验证单调性。

Result: 证明了所提评分在聚类细化下具有单调性；统一了常用的评估标准；在对设置中，同质性-简约性权衡恢复了二元分类器的ROC曲线；展示了在特征选择和算法比较中的实用性。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够联合考虑评分，帮助阐明聚类操作点并识别帕累托最优解，为聚类评估提供了更全面的视角。

Abstract: Scalar metrics are often used to evaluate clusterings against known classes, but they can obscure a fundamental trade-off: clusterings should be informative about class labels while avoiding unnecessary fragmentation. Here we describe normalized scores of cluster homogeneity and parsimony that quantify this trade-off. These scores build on the information bottleneck principle, modified to not reward lossy compression. We show by example and mathematical proof that our definitions of these scores have the intuitive property of varying monotonically under cluster refinement in contrast to related proposals. Extending the information-theoretic framework beyond Shannon entropies, we furthermore derive set-matching and pair-based counterparts of the homogeneity and parsimony scores. These unify commonly used evaluation criteria and show that, in the pair-based setting, the homogeneity-parsimony trade-off recovers the receiver operating characteristic of binary classifiers. We demonstrate the framework's utility for feature selection and algorithm comparison, illustrating how considering scores jointly can clarify clustering operating points and identify Pareto-optimal solutions.

</details>


### [278] [New Complexity-Theoretic Frontiers of Tractability for Neural Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20811)
*Cornelius Brand,Robert Ganian,Mathis Rocton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为线性激活和ReLU激活的神经网络训练问题提供了新的多项式时间可解上界。对于ReLU网络，证明了所有隐藏神经元出度为1的架构可在多项式时间内最优训练；对于线性激活网络，首次识别出满足数据吞吐条件的非平凡多项式时间可解网络架构类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管神经网络在机器学习中至关重要，但最优训练神经网络的计算复杂度仍未完全理解，尤其是在最简单激活函数下。现有研究主要集中于下界改进，而识别新的多项式时间可解网络架构方面进展有限。

Method: 本文通过算法设计提出新的上界结果：1) 针对ReLU网络，扩展了Arora等人的算法，证明所有隐藏神经元出度为1的架构多项式时间可解；2) 针对线性激活网络，基于"数据吞吐条件"（data throughput condition）首次识别非平凡多项式时间可解网络类。

Result: ReLU网络方面，将多项式时间可解范围从之前的结果扩展到所有隐藏神经元出度为1的架构；线性激活网络方面，首次识别出满足数据吞吐条件的非平凡多项式时间可解网络架构类。

Conclusion: 本文在神经网络最优训练的计算复杂度上界方面取得了进展，分别拓展了ReLU网络和线性激活网络的多项式时间可解边界，推进了该领域对可训练网络架构的理解。

Abstract: In spite of the fundamental role of neural networks in contemporary machine learning research, our understanding of the computational complexity of optimally training neural networks remains incomplete even when dealing with the simplest kinds of activation functions. Indeed, while there has been a number of very recent results that establish ever-tighter lower bounds for the problem under linear and ReLU activation functions, less progress has been made towards the identification of novel polynomial-time tractable network architectures. In this article we obtain novel algorithmic upper bounds for training linear- and ReLU-activated neural networks to optimality which push the boundaries of tractability for these problems beyond the previous state of the art. In particular, for ReLU networks we establish the polynomial-time tractability of all architectures where hidden neurons have an out-degree of $1$, improving upon the previous algorithm of Arora, Basu, Mianjy and Mukherjee. On the other hand, for networks with linear activation functions we identify the first non-trivial polynomial-time solvable class of networks by obtaining an algorithm that can optimally train network architectures satisfying a novel data throughput condition.

</details>


### [279] [Robust Asynchronous Q-Learning under Reward and State Corruption via Batching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20822)
*Sreejeet Maity,Aritra Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了BR-Async-Q算法，一种基于批次划分和鲁棒贝尔曼最优算子估计的异步Q-learning方法，能够在奖励和状态观测同时受到对抗性污染的环境中学习最优策略，并首次提供了在此类双重腐败下的鲁棒性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在实际部署中常面临恶意攻击或传感器故障导致的数据污染问题，现有Q-learning算法缺乏对奖励和状态同时被对抗性破坏时的鲁棒性保证。

Method: 提出BR-Async-Q算法，核心包括：(i) 将在线数据流划分为批次以降低方差；(ii) 利用批次数据构建贝尔曼最优算子的鲁棒估计。采用Huber污染模型描述对抗性扰动。

Result: 证明了BR-Async-Q的高概率ℓ∞误差界，与标准Q-learning的误差界相比仅增加一个与污染样本比例相关的可加项；在仅奖励被污染时，误差界对污染比例的依赖达到极小极大最优。

Conclusion: 这是首个为异步Q-learning在奖励和状态同时受污染情况下提供鲁棒性保证的工作，填补了该领域的理论空白。

Abstract: Motivated by reinforcement learning in harsh environments, we consider the problem of learning an optimal policy subject to adversarially corrupted feedback. Specifically, at each time-step, an adversary can perturb both the reward and state observations of the learner following the Huber contamination model. To defend against such data corruption, we propose {\texttt{BR-Async-Q}}: a novel, epoch-based, robust \(Q\)-learning algorithm built upon two key ideas: (i) partitioning the online data stream into batches to reduce variance, and (ii) constructing robust estimates of the Bellman optimality operator using such batched data. We prove a high-probability $\ell_\infty$ error bound for {\texttt{BR-Async-Q}} that matches that for vanilla \(Q\)-learning, up to a small additive term that scales with the fraction of corrupted samples. To our knowledge, this provides the first robustness guarantee for asynchronous \(Q\)-learning subject to both reward and state corruption. Furthermore, when only rewards are corrupted, the dependence of our algorithm's bound on the corruption fraction is minimax optimal.

</details>


### [280] [Beyond Heavy Log Curation: Perplexity-Based APT Detection via Unsupervised, Context-Augmented Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20832)
*Shoya Otsu,Kei Suzuki,Toshiaki Koike-Akino,Jing Liu,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAPTAIN是一种基于困惑度的APT日志检测方法，利用预训练语言模型和最小化预处理，通过上下文增强和困惑度平滑滤波实现高效检测，在降低工程成本的同时达到与强基线竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有APT检测方法依赖大量精心标注的训练数据和复杂的预处理流程，构建和维护这些流程需要大量领域专业知识和工程成本，且难以规模化。

Method: 利用通用预训练语言模型，采用编码器+Q-Former桥接结构编码历史上下文，将紧凑的上下文令牌注入解码器输入，使困惑度反映时间上下文；并应用平滑滤波稳定困惑度时间序列。

Result: 在APT基准测试上，CAPTAIN与现有强基线方法竞争，且在预处理输入质量较低的情况下仍保持鲁棒性，显著降低了日志预处理的开发和运维成本。

Conclusion: CAPTAIN通过最小化领域无关的预处理和上下文增强的困惑度机制，有效降低了APT检测系统的工程开销，同时保持高检测性能。

Abstract: Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs) remain difficult to detect because only a small fraction of events in large-scale logs are attack-related, and investigation is expensive and hard to scale. Prior machine-learning approaches can reduce analyst workload, but they often rely on heavily curated training data and sophisticated preprocessing pipelines. Building and maintaining such pipelines require substantial domain expertise and engineering cost. Motivated by insights from a study of a strong APT detection baseline, we propose CAPTAIN (Context-Augmented Perplexity-based Threat Activity log detectIoN), a perplexity-based detector that leverages general, pre-trained language models with minimal, domain-agnostic preprocessing, enabling robust scoring of long, minimally processed log entries. CAPTAIN encodes recent history with an encoder model and a Q-Former-style bridge, then injects the compact context tokens into the decoder input so that perplexity reflects temporal context. To improve stability, CAPTAIN additionally applies smoothing filters to the perplexity time series. Across APT-oriented benchmarks, CAPTAIN competes with strong existing baselines and remains robust under substantially less curated inputs, that reduces the development and operational cost of advanced log preprocessing.

</details>


### [281] [Offline RL with Hierarchical Action Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20834)
*Ahad Jawaid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiQC通过结合高层潜在规划与低层动作分块，实现了离线目标条件强化学习中长时任务的无偏k步价值备份，在理论上证明了相比标准层次化或平面分块方法更紧的价值误差界，并在OGBench套件中取得了最佳综合表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线目标条件强化学习面临长时任务中的"地平线诅咒"问题，即自举式贝尔曼备份导致价值估计误差沿着长链累积。现有层次化方法虽通过子目标分解任务，但低层控制器存在短视执行和有偏价值估计的问题。

Method: 提出Hierarchical Implicit Q-Chunking (HiQC)算法，核心创新包括：(1) 高层进行潜在规划；(2) 低层采用动作分块，将低层评论家条件化在时域扩展的动作序列上；(3) 实现无偏的k步价值备份，同时在规划层和执行层压缩地平线。

Result: 在OGBench基准测试中，HiQC取得了最高的综合性能，特别是在长时导航任务（如humanoid-giant）上优势最为显著。

Conclusion: HiQC通过高层潜在规划与低层动作分块的双重分解，有效缓解了离线目标条件RL中的地平线诅咒，理论分析证明了这种双重分解在每步备份误差有界模型下能获得比单一层次化或单一分块更紧的价值误差界。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (RL) holds the promise of learning general-purpose policies from static datasets. However, scaling these methods to long-horizon tasks remains a challenge due to the curse of horizon, where value estimation errors can compound through long chains of bootstrapped Bellman backups. Existing hierarchical approaches mitigate this by decomposing tasks into subgoals, yet they often rely on low-level controllers that suffer from myopic execution and biased value estimates. In this work, we propose Hierarchical Implicit Q-Chunking (HiQC), an offline goal-conditioned RL algorithm that combines high-level latent planning with low-level action chunking. By conditioning the low-level critic on temporally extended action sequences, HiQC enables unbiased k-step value backups, compressing the horizon at both the planning and execution levels. We theoretically demonstrate that this dual decomposition results in a tighter bound on value error under a bounded per-backup error model compared to standard hierarchy or flat chunking alone. Empirically, HiQC achieves the highest aggregate performance among the compared methods on the OGBench suite, with its largest gains on long-horizon navigation tasks such as humanoid-giant.

</details>


### [282] [Multilevel Graph Wavelet Compressed Sensing with Scale-Aware Neural Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20857)
*Amirhossein Nouranizadeh,Sarang Rajendra Patil,Alan John Varghese,Varsha Narayanan,Amit Chakraborty,Mengjia Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出图小波压缩感知（GWCS）框架，通过谱图小波变换将图信号表示为稀疏可解释的小波域表示，结合非参数多级重要性采样器和尺度感知图神经网络，实现对图信号的高保真离线压缩，在多个PDE模拟数据集上优于现有基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习方法（如神经算子、物理信息神经网络）训练通常需要大量模拟数据，导致数据准备和模型训练成本高昂，因此需要高效的图信号压缩方法以降低数据存储和传输开销。

Method: 提出GWCS框架：首先利用谱图小波变换将图信号变换到小波域；然后采用非参数多级重要性采样器，在给定压缩比下保留每个尺度内的高能量小波系数；最后使用尺度感知图神经网络从稀疏系数中重建原始信号。

Result: 在合成近似带限图信号和四个PDE模拟数据集（湍流辐射层、粘弹性不稳定性、Kolmogorov流、动态失速）上，GWCS相比图信号采样方法和图自编码器基线，实现了更高的重建保真度和显著的数据压缩。

Conclusion: GWCS框架能够有效压缩图信号，在保持高重建质量的同时大幅减少数据量，为科学机器学习中大规模模拟数据的存储和处理提供了一种高效的离线压缩方案。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning methods such as neural operators and physics-informed neural networks have advanced engineering applications and inverse problems, but their training typically requires large volumes of simulated data. This makes data preparation and model training expensive. We propose Graph Wavelet Compressed Sensing (GWCS), a learning-based framework for offline compression of graph signals by representing them as sparse, interpretable wavelet-domain representations using the spectral graph wavelet transform. The framework combines a nonparametric multilevel importance sampler, which retains high-energy wavelet coefficients within each scale for a given compression ratio, with a scale-aware graph neural network that reconstructs the signal from the sparse coefficients. We evaluate the proposed framework on synthetic approximately band-limited graph signals over random graphs and four PDE simulation datasets over meshes, which include Turbulent Radiative Layer, Viscoelastic Instability, Kolmogorov Flow, and Dynamic Stall. We compare against graph signal sampling methods and graph autoencoder baselines. Results demonstrate that the framework achieves high reconstruction fidelity and substantial data compression compared to existing benchmarks.

</details>


### [283] [Position Bias is Hidden Behind Ceiling Effects: A Permutation Diagnostic for LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20864)
*Hiroki Tamba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 inspect_permute 工具，通过穷举答案排列、卡方/Cramér V 检验及 Bootstrap 置信区间，发现多选 LLM 评估中的位置偏差仅在准确率约 60-95% 的“金发姑娘区间”内可被统计检测，低于此区间受处理负载主导、高于此区间受天花板效应限制；标准 MMLU 中前沿模型均高于检测带，故未检测到偏差不等于无偏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 多选题评估中位置偏差的测量依赖单次答案顺序洗牌，其结果混淆了偏差信号与内容级噪声和采样随机性，缺乏可靠的统计检验方法。

Method: 开发 inspect_permute 工具（inspect_ai 扩展），对每道题穷举所有答案顺序排列，用卡方检验/Cramér V 量化位置偏差，并引入 Bootstrap 置信区间；在 4 个模型（gpt-4o-mini, claude-haiku-4-5, gemini-2.5-flash, grok-3）、5 个 MMLU 子集上执行 24,000 次 API 调用，温度设为 0，且在观测一半数据前通过 SHA-256 哈希预注册证伪预测。

Result: 位置偏差仅当模型准确率处于约 60-95% 区间时才具有统计显著性。低于此区间时，处理负载效应淹没主题信号；高于此区间时，天花板效应压缩方差使其低于卡方检验分辨率。可检测到的偏差分为两种机制：单调 A→D 递减（处理负载型，见于低档模型）和非单调 D-drop（内容模糊型，见于狭窄能力区间）。标准 MMLU 中所有前沿模型准确率均高于检测带。

Conclusion: 标准 MMLU 下前沿模型未检测到位置偏差应解读为“不可测量”而非“无偏”；结合 arXiv:2606.26185 的天花板效应刻画，本文界定了位置偏差测量的可检测区域，使该领域的核心问题可以可验证的形式提出。

Abstract: Position bias in multiple-choice LLM evaluation is widely cited as a confound in capability comparisons, but published measurements rely on single answer-order shuffles whose results confound the bias signal with content-level noise and sampling stochasticity. I introduce inspect_permute, an open-source extension to the inspect_ai evaluation framework that runs exhaustive answer-order permutations per question and reports the chi-squared / Cramer V signature of position bias with bootstrap confidence intervals. I apply the tool across four vendors (gpt-4o-mini, claude-haiku-4-5, gemini-2.5-flash, grok-3) on five MMLU subjects, 24,000 API calls under temperature-0 generation, with falsifier predictions pre-registered via a public SHA-256 hash before half the data was observed. Position bias turns out to be statistically detectable only within a roughly 60-95% base-accuracy Goldilocks zone. Below it, processing-load dominance swamps subject-specific signal; above it, ceiling effects compress the variance below the chi-squared test resolution. Detectable cells separate into two mechanism types: monotone A-to-D decrease (processing_load, in low-tier models) and non-monotone D-drop (content_ambiguity, in a narrow capability band). Standard MMLU places every frontier-tier model above the detection band, so absence of signal there should be read as not measurable, not unbiased. Together with the ceiling-effect characterisation in arXiv:2606.26185, this work brackets the detectable region of position-bias measurement and makes the field central question askable in a verifiable form. Package, data, preregistration under MIT.

</details>


### [284] [TwistedMerge: Certified Higher-Order Diagnostics and Abstention for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20887)
*Ting Gong,Shitan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TwistedMerge框架，将模型合并形式化为有限下降问题，通过循环一致性、上同调检验等严格测试来认证合并质量，无法通过测试时选择弃权并回退到普通或同步化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型合并方法中，两两可对齐性并不保证全局一致性对齐，缺乏可靠的认证机制来判断合并是否真正有效。

Method: 将合并形式化为有限下降问题，检查点作为局部对象，对齐映射作为转移，循环积作为残差。TwistedMerge管道包含固定图平均、可移除规范不一致性、指定比较复形上的认证中心障碍以及非阿贝尔和乐四部分。残差需通过逆一致性、系数识别、中心性和闭包测试后才提升为上同调类，否则弃权。

Result: 证明了常数边不可行结果、冻结复形三路和前声明族误差控制定理。植入了神经对齐缺陷后通过循环一致性同步移除。受控中央系统恢复预测的非上同调边和射影秩行为。低秩适配器审计表明朴素因子平均依赖于GLr代表选择，而全局因子同步和稠密delta SVD稳定。自然检查点集合上，循环残差不预测合并退化，无自然中心或周期指标类被认证。

Conclusion: 将下降理论定位为可证伪的认证与弃权框架，为模型合并提供了严格的数学基础和安全保障机制。

Abstract: Model merging combines independently trained or fine-tuned models, but pairwise alignability does not imply globally consistent alignment. We formulate merging as a finite descent problem in which checkpoints are local objects, alignment maps are transitions, and cycle products are residuals. TwistedMerge is a conservative certification pipeline that separates fixed-chart averaging, synchronization-removable gauge inconsistency, a certified central obstruction on a specified comparison complex, and nonabelian holonomy. A residual is promoted to a cohomology class only after inverse-consistency, coefficient-identification, centrality, and closure tests; otherwise the method abstains and returns an ordinary or synchronized fallback. We prove a constant-edge no-go result, frozen-complex three-way and predeclared-family error-control theorems, and a refinement test for comparison-complex sensitivity. A planted neural alignment defect is removed by cycle-consistent synchronization, showing that a nonzero cycle score alone is not a higher obstruction. Controlled central systems recover the predicted non-coboundary and projective-rank behavior, while noisy estimates move from certification to abstention without false lifts on the tested controls. A trained low-rank-adapter audit shows that naive factor averaging depends on the chosen GLr representative, whereas global factor synchronization and dense-delta SVD are stable. On natural checkpoint collections, cycle residuals do not predict merge degradation and no natural central or period-index class is certified.
