We have formulated a sequential multi-stage optimization problem in which semantically aligned tasks are clustered once after a short initial training phase, and then end-to-end (E2E) joint training is conducted exclusively within the discovered groups. Specifically, the problem decomposes into two stages: (i) a semantic clustering problem leveraging hierarchical density-based spatial clustering, and (ii) an intra-cluster E2E CMT-SemCom learning problem. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively mitigates destructive cooperation and negative transfer, yielding accuracy gains compared to unclustered multi-tasking and individual training baselines.

</details>


### [320] [Context-weighted Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21427)
*Daniil Cherniavskii,Daniel Severo,Karen Ullrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于上下文加权的离散流匹配方法，通过修改连续时间马尔可夫链和重新加权损失函数，在OpenWebText上将生成困惑度降低高达63%，且计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准因子化训练目标让模型同时面对易预测的低熵令牌和模糊的高熵令牌，导致训练信号质量不均。作者观察到令牌的不确定性与其局部上下文的密度密切相关，由此出发探索利用局部上下文信息改进离散生成建模。

Method: 1) 提出上下文加权的连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）采样器，在生成过程中融入局部上下文信息；2) 设计缩放交叉熵损失函数，根据令牌的局部上下文密度重新加权训练信号，使模型更关注信息量更大的令牌。

Result: 1) 上下文加权采样器在几乎零计算开销下提升生成质量；2) 缩放交叉熵损失在OpenWebText上将生成困惑度降低高达63%；3) 在生成质量上匹配强半自回归块扩散基线，同时保留了任意顺序生成的能力。

Conclusion: 局部上下文是离散生成建模中的关键因素，简单的上下文感知改进即可显著提升采样效率和训练效率。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching provides a flexible framework for generative modeling on discrete structures. However, the standard factorized training objective exposes the model to targets of varying difficulty, mixing well-conditioned, predictable tokens with ambiguous, high-entropy ones. We empirically demonstrate that the uncertainty over the value of each token is closely related to the density of available context in its neighborhood. Motivated by this observation, we propose a simple modification to the underlying continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) that incorporates local context information. Our context-weighted sampler improves generation quality with negligible computational overhead, while our scaled cross-entropy loss function reweights the training signal from different tokens and reduces generative perplexity by up to 63% on OpenWebText. Moreover, our approach matches a strong semi-autoregressive block diffusion baseline in quality while retaining the ability to perform generation in any order. These results highlight the role of local context as an important factor in discrete generative modeling and show that simple context-aware modifications can significantly improve both sampling and training efficiency.

</details>


### [321] [KroQuant: Kronecker-Structured Block Transforms for Efficient Post-Training Quantization of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21446)
*Yann Bouquet,Alireza Khodamoradi,Kristof Denolf,Mathieu Salzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KroQuant是一种针对扩散Transformer（DiT）的后训练量化方法，通过Kronecker结构可逆变换对激活值进行逐块（32元素）处理，结合离线LoRaQ权重校准，在W4A4量化下实现比SVDQuant和LoRaQ更优的图像质量，且量化核比SmoothQuant快14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer（DiT）在W4A4后训练量化（PTQ）中，激活值存在异常值导致4-bit格式无法准确表示，现有方案在量化质量与推理成本之间存在权衡：SmoothQuant计算廉价但量化精度差，固定Hadamard变换需大块大小导致高在线成本，全维度可逆变换校准最优但带来昂贵的密集矩阵乘法开销。

Method: 提出KroQuant方法，对激活值每32元素块施加学习的Kronecker结构可逆变换，参数量少于per-channel scaling的一半；块局部结构以小张量核GEMM运行；结合离线LoRaQ权重校准吸收残差量化误差。

Result: 在MI350 GPU上，KroQuant量化核比SmoothQuant核快14%；在PixArt-Σ、SANA、FLUX.1-schnell模型上W4A4（MXFP4e2）量化下，在MJHQ-30K和SDCI数据集上比SVDQuant和LoRaQ更接近FP参考输出，同时保持或提升图像质量。

Conclusion: KroQuant通过Kronecker结构可逆变换在量化精度与推理效率之间取得了优于现有方案（SmoothQuant、固定Hadamard变换、全维度可逆变换）的权衡。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) of diffusion transformers (DiTs) to W4A4 severely degrades output quality, because activations entering each linear layer contain outliers that 4-bit formats cannot represent. The standard fix applies an invertible linear transform to the activations and its inverse to the weights before quantizing both. Normalization layers between blocks force this transform to run online at every denoising step, making its inference computation cost the binding design constraint. Existing options trade quantization quality for inference cost: per-channel scaling (SmoothQuant) is computationally cheap but impacts the magnitude of the channels, which can harm quantization accuracy; fixed Hadamard transforms yield better quantization accuracy but require large block sizes that incur a high online cost; learned full-$d$ invertible transforms calibrate best but entail a prohibitive dense $d \times d$ matrix multiplication (GEMM) per layer per step.
  We propose KroQuant, a PTQ method that applies a learned Kronecker-structured invertible transform to each 32-element block of the activation, storing less than half the parameters of per-channel scaling. The block-local structure runs as small tensor-core GEMMs, and on an MI350 GPU the KroQuant quantizer kernel is up to $14\%$ faster than the SmoothQuant kernel. Offline LoRaQ weight calibration then absorbs the residual per-weight quantization error. On PixArt-$Σ$, SANA, and FLUX.1-schnell at W4A4 (MXFP4e2), KroQuant produces outputs closer to the FP reference than SVDQuant and LoRaQ on MJHQ-30K and SDCI, while preserving or improving image quality.

</details>


### [322] [Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21453)
*Rajiv Shailesh Chitale,Rahul Madhavan,Taneesh Gupta,Deepanway Ghosal,Aravindan Raghuveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TTEL，一种利用环境反馈进行token级错误定位的推理时扩展算法，通过比较条件概率识别错误步骤、截断轨迹并分支生成新内容，最大化复用有效前缀。实验表明，TTEL在顺序推理任务中建立了严格占优的帕累托边界，在LiveCodeBench上用Qwen3-8B以一半的token生成量达到71.0%的pass@64，并在数学基准上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时扩展方法（如独立采样、多轮精炼）缺乏token级别的信用分配，导致有效推理前缀被频繁丢弃，计算效率低下。本文旨在通过细粒度的错误定位来提升推理时计算效率。

Method: TTEL利用固定反馈或环境反馈进行token级错误定位：在知情反馈与空上下文基线之间比较条件概率，隔离错误发生的步骤；然后截断该轨迹并在该点分支生成新的内容，最大化复用已有的有效前缀。

Result: 在多个顺序推理任务中，TTEL建立了严格占优的帕累托边界（pass-at-k vs. 生成token成本）。具体地，在LiveCodeBench上使用Qwen3-8B，TTEL达到71.0%的pass@64，生成约360.4k token，而独立采样需735.0k token（约一半）。在AIME-2025和HMMT-2025数学基准上，TTEL在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507上均显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: TTEL通过token级错误定位实现了高效的推理时计算扩展，显著减少了无效token生成，在代码和数学推理任务上均展现了严格的性能优势。

Abstract: Scaling inference-time computation has emerged as a reliable method to improve the performance of large language models on complex reasoning and programming tasks. However, standard approaches such as independent sampling and sequential multi-turn refinement operate without token-level credit assignment, resulting in computational inefficiency, since valid reasoning prefixes are frequently discarded. In this work, we introduce Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization (TTEL), an inference-time algorithm that utilizes fixed or environment feedback to perform token-level error localization. By comparing conditional probabilities under informed feedback against a null-context baseline, TTEL isolates the step at which an error occurred. The algorithm then truncates the trajectory and branches a new generation, maximally reusing the valid prefix. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that TTEL establishes strictly dominating Pareto frontiers across sequential reasoning domains, measured by pass-at-k vs. generated-token cost. With Qwen3-8B on LiveCodeBench, TTEL attains a pass@64 of 71.0% while generating approximately half as many tokens as independent sampling (360.4k vs. 735.0k). Generalizing to math benchmarks AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025, TTEL cleanly outperforms competing test-time baselines across both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507.

</details>


### [323] [Error Certificates for KV-Cache Eviction via Randomized Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21475)
*Peng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明确定性KV缓存淘汰无法估计注意力误差，并提出随机化淘汰方法，通过泊松采样和Hájek校正实现每个时间步的误差认证（经验覆盖率达0.97），同时实验表明随机化带来的是归因能力而非预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的确定性KV缓存淘汰（保留top-k重要token）无法知道被丢弃的内容对注意力输出误差的影响，缺乏可证明的误差估计。作者旨在设计一种可识别、可认证的KV缓存淘汰方法。

Method: 1) 理论证明确定性淘汰无法一致估计注意力输出误差；2) 提出随机化淘汰：对尾部token进行泊松采样，利用已知包含概率进行Hájek校正（在softmax内加一个logit偏移），并基于调查抽样方差估计构建每步误差证书；3) 在真实负载上进行七个预注册实验。

Result: 1) 误差证书在无精度损失下达到0.97经验覆盖率；2) 七个预注册声明中三个未通过：问题感知淘汰在25-50%预算下近乎免费、输出对数概率比证书更能预测失败、证书门控的预算升级无收益；3) 归因能力保留：证书能区分缓存引入的失败与固有失败（AUC 0.73-0.75 vs 输出置信度的0.47-0.54），比随机或置信度门控更好地调度重计算。

Conclusion: 随机化淘汰的核心价值在于归因（attribution）而非预测（prediction）：它不能完美预测失败，但能为缓存引起的错误提供可审计的认证信号，从而指导重计算调度。

Abstract: Deterministic KV-cache eviction keeps the top-$k$ tokens under an importance score and deletes the rest. We prove that this design cannot know what it destroyed: evicted values can be altered so that everything the serving system retains is unchanged while the true attention-output error grows arbitrarily, so no serving-time estimator of that error is consistent. Randomized eviction restores identifiability. With a Poisson-sampled tail at known inclusion probabilities, one logit offset performs the Hájek correction inside the softmax, and a survey-sampling variance estimator over the retained set becomes a per-step error certificate with 0.97 empirical coverage at no accuracy cost. On real workloads we pre-registered seven claims and lost three: question-aware eviction at 25--50\% budgets is nearly free; output log-probability predicts failure better than the certificate; certificate-gated budget escalation adds nothing. What survives is attribution: the certificate separates cache-induced from inherent failures (AUC 0.73--0.75, against 0.47--0.54 for output confidence) and schedules recomputation better than random or confidence gating. Randomization buys attribution, not prediction.

</details>


### [324] [Finite-Sample Coverage Audits for High-Recall Candidate Generation: Certification and Learning-Theoretic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21480)
*Martin Anthony,Kaveh Salehzadeh Nobari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在高召回率初步筛选（候选集生成）阶段后，如何通过有限样本审计来认证被排除池中遗漏的相关项目数量。核心发现是：仅标注候选集内部无法认证遗漏量，必须对排除池进行采样；且任何有效审计在无遗漏时需标注约 N_0/m 个排除池样本才能认证遗漏少于 m 个。基于此，作者开发了一套精确有限样本的统计工具（利用二项/超几何分布反演），用于认证遗漏质量、换算召回率、同时认证多个候选生成器，并生成压力测试证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高召回率→低精度流水线中，第一阶段的候选集生成器会漏掉相关项目，这些漏掉的项目在后续所有阶段都无法被恢复。现有方法缺乏对遗漏质量进行有限样本有效认证的理论保证，即无法以统计上严格的方式回答"我们到底漏了多少相关项？"这一问题。

Method: 理论方面：证明仅标注候选集无法认证遗漏量（下界），并给出排除池采样量的匹配下界（N_0/m量级）。方法方面：构建基于二项分布和超几何分布精确反演的有限样本审计框架，包括两池设计（候选池+排除池）下的遗漏质量认证、召回率换算、多候选生成器联合认证及压力测试。

Result: （1）证明仅使用候选集内部标签无法对遗漏质量进行任何非平凡认证；（2）推导出排除池审计所需标签数量的匹配下界：为认证遗漏少于 m 个，在无遗漏时需标注约 N_0/m 个排除池样本；（3）排除池审计是极小化最优策略；（4）开发了完整的精确有限样本审计工具箱。

Conclusion: 排除池审计不仅是方便的选择，更是遗漏质量认证在零遗漏情形下的极小化最优策略。所提出的有限样本工具包可以在不依赖渐近近似的情况下，为实际流水线提供严格的统计保证，并可用于选择满足遗漏质量目标的最优候选生成器。

Abstract: An initial high-recall stage in an empirical pipeline decides which items pass to later review, labelling, or modelling, and relevant items it misses are lost to every subsequent stage. We study how many audit labels are needed to certify, with finite-sample validity, that this missed relevant mass is small, and our main results characterise the label complexity of this problem. We first show that no procedure using only labels from inside the candidate set can certify any non-trivial bound on the missed mass: the audit must sample the excluded pool, the only region where unrecovered relevant items can lie. We then prove a matching finite-corpus lower bound. Any valid audit that certifies fewer than $m$ missed relevant items with high probability when none are present, even if adaptive and permitted to label the entire included pool, must inspect on the order of $N_0/m$ excluded-pool labels. Excluded-pool auditing is therefore minimax rate-optimal, not merely convenient, for missed-mass certification in the zero-miss regime. Building on this characterisation, we develop an exact finite-sample toolkit, using binomial and hypergeometric inversion rather than asymptotic approximation, that certifies missed mass, converts it to recall through a two-pool design, certifies pre-specified families of nested candidate generators simultaneously, and produces stress-test certificates against declared perturbation mechanisms. These certificates can be paired with observable review burden to select the least burdensome pre-specified candidate generator meeting a missed-mass target. Every guarantee holds under one discipline: the candidate generator, or the pre-specified family from which it is selected, and the audit rule are fixed before the certification labels are examined.

</details>


### [325] [Windowed-MTP: Removing the Full-Context Draft-KV Tax at Million-Token Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21535)
*Alagappan Valliappan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Windowed-MTP，一种无需训练、即插即用的方法，通过对多头令牌预测（MTP）草稿头的注意力机制施加滑动窗口+注意力汇聚（类似StreamingLLM），将草稿的KV工作集限定为常数，在百万级上下文下减少约99%的KV条目，同时保持完整注意力验证不变，从而消除大规模上下文中MTP草稿头的线性读取成本。在三类架构（Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B、Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B）的单GPU实验中，该方法将每个解码步骤的草稿成本降低28%-44%，且可回收未读取的草稿KV（占百万上下文中总KV的7.7-11%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长上下文（百万令牌级）场景下，标准的多头令牌预测（MTP/NEXTN）草稿头的注意力机制需要完整读取整个KV缓存，导致草稿成本随上下文线性增长，抵消甚至逆转推测解码带来的加速收益。该问题在混合注意力/线性注意力目标模型（验证成本更低）中更为严重。

Method: 对MTP草稿头的注意力机制单独应用StreamingLLM风格的滑动窗口+注意力汇聚（attention sink），即只保留最近的滑动窗口令牌和少量初始令牌，将KV工作集限定为常数。完整注意力的验证模型保持不变。该方法无需训练，可即插即用，且推理无损——验证模型决定最终接受的令牌，窗口化仅改变提议的候选令牌。

Result: 在三类架构（Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B、Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B）的单GPU百万上下文实验中，Windowed-MTP将每个解码步骤的草稿成本降低28%-44%，该优势与输入无关且随上下文增长而扩大。在匹配接受率下，端到端解码延迟相应改善。同时可回收未读取的草稿KV（占百万上下文中总KV的7.7-11%），且不影响接受率或输出质量。

Conclusion: Windowed-MTP通过限制草稿头的注意力范围，有效解决了长上下文场景下MTP推测解码的草稿成本瓶颈问题，在保持输出分布不变的前提下实现了显著的加速效果。该方法简单、高效、实用，特别适合部署于长上下文推理系统。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by having a cheap draft propose tokens that a target verifies in parallel. Frontier models increasingly ship a built-in Multi-Token-Prediction (MTP/NEXTN) draft head under the assumption that the draft is negligibly cheap. At million-token context this breaks: an MTP draft head typically runs full attention over the entire KV cache at every draft step, so its read grows linearly with context and comes to dominate the draft cost -- precisely where speculation is most valuable. The effect compounds with draft length (a deep native draft can turn net-negative, slower than no speculation) and sharpens under hybrid/linear-attention targets, where cheaper verification leaves the draft's full-attention read exposed. We apply a StreamingLLM-style sliding window plus attention sink to the draft's attention only (Windowed-MTP), leaving full-attention verification intact. It is training-free, drop-in, and lossless by construction: the full-attention target still decides every accepted token, so windowing changes only which tokens are proposed, never which are accepted. It bounds the draft's KV working set to a constant, dropping ~99% of KV entries at 1M. Across three architecture families (Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B and a Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B) at 1M context on a single GPU in SGLang, windowing cuts the per-decode-step cost over the shipping native MTP draft by +28% to +44%, an input-invariant margin that widens with context. Since per-token latency is this cost divided by acceptance length, at matched acceptance end-to-end decode latency improves by the same amount, and more where windowing also lifts acceptance, while preserving the target's verified output distribution. Finally, the unread draft KV -- 7.7-11% of total KV at 1M -- is reclaimed via a compact ring buffer at no acceptance or quality cost.

</details>


### [326] [Zero-Flow Two-Sample Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21542)
*Yakun Wang,Leyang Wang,Song Liu,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于零流判别准则的两样本检验方法（ZF2ST），通过学习局部错位方向模式来区分分布差异，同时保证统计有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两样本检验中，现有方法在检测结构化分布变化时存在检验力不足或类型-I错误校准不佳的问题，需要一种既能利用灵活神经网络又保持有效统计校准的方法。

Method: 基于零流判别准则（ZFD）构建统计差异量，将见证学习与假设评估分离，分别开发了基于回归和最大化检验力的两种见证学习方法。

Result: 在合成数据和图像数据集上，ZF2ST在检测结构化分布变化时表现出强检验力，同时保持了良好的类型-I错误校准。

Conclusion: ZF2ST通过分离见证学习与假设评估，实现了神经网络灵活性与统计有效性的兼得，为结构化分布变化的两样本检验提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We propose a new approach to two-sample testing for deciding whether two sets of samples are drawn from the same distribution. The test is built on a statistical discrepancy based on the zero-flow criterion, termed zero-flow discrepancy (ZFD). We prove the validity of ZFD and propose a practical testing procedure, termed the zero-flow two-sample test (ZF2ST). The key idea is to learn how samples from the two distributions are locally misaligned and use the resulting directional pattern as evidence of distributional difference. By separating witness learning from hypothesis evaluation, ZF2ST can use flexible neural networks while maintaining valid statistical calibration. We develop both regression-based and power-maximized approaches for learning the witness. Experiments on synthetic and image datasets demonstrate that ZF2ST can achieve strong testing power for structured distributional changes while maintaining well-calibrated type-I error.

</details>


### [327] [X$^3$-OPD: Distilling Reasoning into Large Audio-Language Models via On-Policy Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21550)
*Dongjie Fu,Di Cao,Xize Cheng,Zihan Zhang,Wenxu Jia,Yifu Chen,Shengpeng Ji,Yu Zhang,Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出X$^3$-OPD跨模态同策略蒸馏框架，通过文本教师模型指导音频-语言学生模型进行深度逻辑推理，在三类对称语料上训练，显著提升音频推理能力与思维链质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频-语言模型在听觉感知上取得进展，但在深度逻辑推理方面落后于纯文本大语言模型，主要原因是缺乏高质量的音频推理数据。

Method: 提出X$^3$-OPD跨模态同策略蒸馏框架：学生模型基于自身声学感知生成推理轨迹，教师模型使用匹配文本输入和验证答案提供token级指导。构建三层对称语料：文本推理语音渲染、复杂声场景音频事件推理、涉及副语言线索的口语对话推理。

Result: 在MMSU、MMAU、BIG Bench Audio和MMAR基准上，X$^3$-OPD显著提升了音频推理和思维链质量，同时在域偏移下较好地保留了模型现有能力。

Conclusion: 跨模态蒸馏可超越文本可恢复内容，扩展到基于非语言事件、韵律和对话语境的推理，有效弥合音频-语言模型与文本模型在推理能力上的差距。

Abstract: While large audio-language models have achieved remarkable progress in auditory perception, they still lag behind text-based large language models in deep logical reasoning, primarily due to the scarcity of high-quality audio reasoning data. To bridge this gap, we propose X$^3$-OPD, a cross-modal on-policy distillation framework that transfers reasoning capabilities from a powerful text teacher to an audio-language student. During training, the student generates reasoning trajectories conditioned on its own acoustic perception, while the teacher provides token-level guidance using matched textual inputs and verified answers. We further construct a three-tier symmetric corpus covering textual reasoning rendered into speech, audio-event reasoning grounded in complex acoustic scenes, and spoken-dialogue reasoning involving paralinguistic cues. This design extends cross-modal distillation beyond textually recoverable content to reasoning grounded in non-linguistic events, prosody, and conversational context. Experiments on MMSU, MMAU, BIG Bench Audio, and MMAR demonstrate that X$^3$-OPD substantially improves audio-grounded reasoning and chain-of-thought quality while largely preserving the model's existing capabilities under domain shift.

</details>


### [328] [Graph Learning on Ensembles of Cyclic Peptides: An Investigation of Molecular Ensemble Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21561)
*Aaron Feller,Kris Deibler,Maxim Secor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 EnsembleEGNN，一种分子构象集成基础模型，通过共享EGNN编码每个构象并用Set Attention池化，在环肽数据集CREMP上预训练。相比从头训练（R²=0.005），预训练模型显著提升（R²=0.477，Pearson r=0.699），与BERT序列编码器联合训练后进一步优化（R²=0.538，Pearson r=0.737），证明编码构象集成可改善环肽性质预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子性质预测通常仅使用单一代表性构象，忽略了分子在溶液中以构象集成形式存在的事实，导致信息丢失和预测性能受限。

Method: 提出EnsembleEGNN，先通过共享EGNN层分别编码每个构象，再用Set Attention Block池化所有构象表征；在CREMP环肽构象集成数据集上使用掩码token恢复、噪声坐标重构和成对距离重构三种自监督任务进行多任务预训练。

Result: 从头训练时R²=0.005完全失效；预训练后R²=0.477、Pearson r=0.699，优于序列BERT基线（R²=0.439, r=0.667）；与BERT联合端到端训练进一步提升至R²=0.538、Pearson r=0.737。

Conclusion: 将构象集成编码为单一热力学信息嵌入可显著提升环肽性质预测性能，验证了集成构象建模相比单一构象或纯序列方法的优势。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction from structure often uses a single representative conformation, even though many molecules exist as conformational ensembles in solution. We introduce EnsembleEGNN, a molecular ensemble foundation model that encodes an ensemble by first encoding each conformer with shared Equivariant Graph Neural Network (EGNN) layers, then pooling the resulting conformer representations with a Set Attention Block. We pretrain the model on CREMP, a cyclic peptide ensemble dataset, using a multi-task self-supervised objective combining masked token recovery, noisy-coordinate reconstruction, and pairwise distance reconstruction. On the CREMP-CycPeptMPDB dataset, training EnsembleEGNN from scratch fails entirely ($R^2=0.005$). However, the pretrained model reaches $R^2=0.477$ and Pearson $r=0.699$, outperforming the sequence-only BERT baseline ($R^2=0.439$, Pearson $r=0.667$). When EnsembleEGNN is co-trained end-to-end with the BERT sequence encoder, the hybrid model improves further to $R^2=0.538$ and Pearson $r=0.737$. These results demonstrate that encoding conformational ensembles into a single thermodynamically informed embedding improves cyclic-peptide property prediction.

</details>


### [329] [Beyond Sufficiency: Time Series Explanation with Counterfactual Necessity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21573)
*Hongnan Ma,Yiwei Shi,Mengyue Yang,Weiru Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TimePNS框架，通过反事实必要性机制识别时间序列分类器中既充分又必要的关键子序列，采用两阶段设计：第一阶段学习可识别的因果生成过程和充分性掩码，第二阶段通过反事实干预生成必要性信号来优化解释掩码，在合成和真实数据上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向充分性的时间序列解释方法可能将支持预测但不必要的虚假子序列赋予高重要性，缺乏对必要性的考量，导致解释不够精确。

Method: 两阶段框架：Stage I学习可识别的因果生成过程与充分性导向的解释掩码；Stage II对时间因子进行反事实干预以获取必要性信号，监督时间门控机制优化初始解释，抑制非必要成分并强调反事实必要的成分。

Result: 在合成和真实世界时间序列基准上，TimePNS更准确地识别决策关键子序列，并在充分性-必要性权衡上持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 将反事实必要性引入时间序列可解释性是有效的，两阶段设计能够同时兼顾解释的充分性和必要性，提升解释的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Faithful explanations of time-series classifiers should identify subsequences that are not only sufficient to preserve a black-box model's prediction, but also necessary for maintaining it. However, existing sufficiency-oriented methods can assign high importance to spurious subsequences that support the prediction without being essential to the model's decision. We introduce \textbf{TimePNS}, a necessity-aware framework for time-series explanation. Inspired by Pearl's counterfactual notion of necessity, TimePNS assesses whether a temporal factor is necessary by intervening on it and measuring whether the original prediction is disrupted. The framework adopts a two-stage design. Stage I learns an identifiable causal generative process together with a sufficiency-oriented explanation mask. Stage II performs counterfactual interventions on temporal factors to derive necessity signals, which supervise a temporal gate that refines the initial explanation by suppressing non-essential components and emphasizing counterfactually necessary ones. Experiments on synthetic and real-world time-series benchmarks show that TimePNS more accurately identifies decision-critical subsequences and consistently improves sufficiency-necessity trade-offs over strong baselines.

</details>


### [330] [Expanding Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21585)
*Sophia Tang,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出扩展生成流（EFlows）和扩展流映射（EFMs），通过逐步增广状态空间的插值方法，使流模型能够处理可变维度/序列长度的生成任务，并在连续与离散模态上实现可控的变尺寸生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的生成模型受限于固定维度或固定序列长度，无法处理输出规模本身作为可学习自由度的场景，亟需一种能动态扩展状态空间的生成框架。

Method: 定义沿扩展插值器（expanding interpolant）增长的流，将状态空间通过条件噪声逐步增广；在此基础上提出扩展流映射，分解为扩展算子（expand operator）和传输映射（transport map）两个可学习操作，通过组合实现联合扩展与去噪。该框架还扩展至离散单纯形，支持变尺寸图生成和变长序列生成。

Result: 在连续和离散模态上验证了EFlows和EFMs的有效性，表明其能处理输出尺寸作为可学习自由度的生成任务，并证明现有固定画布流方法为其特例（扩展算子为单位映射时）。

Conclusion: EFlows和EFMs为输出尺寸本身可学习的变尺寸生成提供了一个原则性框架，统一并推广了现有固定尺寸流模型。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models have enabled remarkable progress in fast and controllable generation across continuous and discrete state spaces, yet existing parameterizations are constrained to fixed dimensions or fixed sequence lengths. Here, we introduce Expanding Generative Flows (EFlows), which define flows between distributions of increasing dimensionality along an expanding interpolant that grows the state by augmenting it with conditional noise. Building on this construction, we propose Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs), a new class of flow maps that distill the expanding interpolant into efficient few-step generative models. Each EFM factors the map between any two timesteps into two learnable operations: an expand operator, which augments the state space with new coordinates or tokens conditioned on the current state, and a transport map, which pushes the expanded state forward along the interpolant. Composing these operators yields a single map that jointly expands and denoises the state, recovering existing fixed-canvas flows and flow maps as the special case in which the expand operator is the identity. We further extend the framework to the discrete simplex, enabling variable-size graph generation and variable-length sequence generation. Across both continuous and discrete modalities, we establish EFlows and EFMs as a principled framework for settings in which output size is itself a learned, controllable degree of freedom.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [331] [HERMES: Heterogeneous Edge-Relational Multi-Head Embedded SSM Attention for Traffic Conflict Prediction at Signalized Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20505)
*Md Monzurul Islam,Subasish Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HERMES模型，利用异构图神经网络与时序学习对信号交叉口的交通冲突进行场景级分类评估，在零样本迁移场景下仍保持高检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通冲突评估方法多将多智能体场景扁平化为固定特征向量或仅评估独立二元交互，难以捕捉异质性交互结构与场景级风险的动态演化，亟需一种能保留异构交互拓扑与连续安全语义的建模方法。

Method: 将交通冲突评估建模为时序异质场景图分类问题，提出HERMES：基于异质边关系图神经网络，集成SSM驱动的多头注意力机制，包含异质节点（车辆/行人）、关系特定边（含连续运动学与替代安全度量描述）、关系特定注意力、动态节点-边更新、安全感知图池化及时序序列学习。

Result: 在109,028条轨迹序列数据集上，HERMES取得AUC-ROC 0.9898、AUC-PR 0.9412、F1 0.8449；5%虚警率下检测到95.7%冲突序列；零样本外推测试AUC-ROC 0.9752、AUC-PR 0.7829，联合源-目标训练可进一步提升目标域表现。

Conclusion: 保留异构交互拓扑结构、安全信息驱动的边语义及短期时序演化能显著提升场景级冲突分类性能，支持信号交叉口的可迁移路侧安全监测。

Abstract: Surrogate safety measures (SSMs) enable proactive traffic safety assessment, but many existing methods evaluate pairwise interactions independently or flatten multi-agent scenes into fixed feature vectors, limiting their ability to represent heterogeneous interaction structure and evolving scene-level risk. This study formulates traffic conflict assessment as temporal heterogeneous scene-graph classification and proposes HERMES, a heterogeneous edge-relational graph neural network with SSM-informed multi-head attention. Vehicles and pedestrians are represented as heterogeneous nodes, while vehicle-vehicle, vehicle-pedestrian, and pedestrian-pedestrian interactions are encoded as relation-specific edges with continuous kinematic and surrogate-safety descriptors. Relation-specific attention, dynamic node-edge updates, safety-aware graph pooling, and temporal sequence learning are jointly used to estimate scene-level conflict probability. HERMES was evaluated using 109,028 trajectory-derived sequences from a signalized urban intersection and tested on an independently collected comparable intersection dataset. Enhanced HERMES achieved an AUC-ROC of 0.9898 +/- 0.0013, an AUC-PR of 0.9412 +/- 0.0067, and an F1 score of 0.8449 +/- 0.0103. At a 5% false-alarm rate, it detected 95.7% of conflict sequences, outperforming the strongest Transformer baseline and XGBoost. In zero-shot external evaluation, HERMES achieved an AUC-ROC of 0.9752 and an AUC-PR of 0.7829. Joint source-target training further improved target-site performance with limited target-site data. These findings show that preserving heterogeneous interaction topology, safety-informed edge semantics, and short-term temporal evolution improves scene-level conflict classification and supports transferable roadside safety monitoring at signalized intersections.

</details>


### [332] [PhysCoRe: Physics-Corrected Residual World Models for Material-Aware Deformable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20653)
*Haocheng Yin,Shuohan Tao,Yongsheng Chen,Lu Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysCoRe是一个将可微分的物质点法(MPM)模拟器与两个前馈神经网络耦合的物理校正残差世界模型。它通过材料精炼模块(MfM)从视觉观测中推断每粒子弹性，通过残差校正模块(RfD)学习并校正模拟器无法捕捉的系统性偏差，在真实可变形物体操作序列上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可变形物体操作预测方法存在两类问题：基于每物体优化拟合材料参数的方法速度慢且无法泛化，而端到端学习方法外推性差且常违反基本物理结构。需要一个既能保持物理一致性又能高效泛化的预测模型。

Method: PhysCoRe由三部分组成：1) 可微分MPM模拟器作为物理基础；2) Material from Motion (MfM)模块从视觉观测推断每粒子弹性参数；3) Residual from Dynamics (RfD)模块学习并校正模拟器内部动力学的偏差。此外支持在线材料辨识，利用MfM的预测不确定性引导探索。

Result: 在真实可变形物体操作序列上，PhysCoRe在预测精度上优于现有最优基线方法，且其预测的置信度在物体几何上形成可靠分布，为未来置信度引导的探索提供自然信号。

Conclusion: 结合可微分物理模拟器与数据驱动残差学习的方法能有效兼顾物理一致性与泛化能力，且模型的不确定性估计可为主动探索提供指导。

Abstract: Predicting how deformable objects evolve under robotic manipulation is a longstanding challenge. Existing approaches typically rely on per-object optimization to fit material parameters, which can be slow and cannot generalize, while end-to-end learned alternatives extrapolate poorly and often violate basic physical structure. We present PhysCoRe, a physics-corrected residual world model that couples a differentiable Material Point Method (MPM) simulator with two feed-forward neural networks. A material refinement module, Material from Motion (MfM), infers per-particle elasticity from visual observations, grounding the simulator in object-specific physics. A residual correction module, Residual from Dynamics (RfD), learns the discrepancy and predicts corrections to the simulator's internal dynamics, absorbing systematic biases that the analytical model cannot capture. This design also supports online material identification on novel objects. MfM adapts from limited interactions, and its predictive uncertainty steers further exploration toward the regions where its estimate is least confident. Experiments on real deformable-object manipulation sequences show that PhysCoRe outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in prediction accuracy, and that its predicted confidence forms a reliable distribution across the object's geometry, providing a natural signal for future confidence-guided exploration.

</details>


### [333] [Scalable Low-Cost Laboratory Automation: A Digital Twin-Integrated Robotic Platform for Autonomous Liquid Handling (RAINBOTTM)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20662)
*Mohamed Rami Ayeche,Souhil Sid,Ahyen Mostofa,Rehaan Hussain,Ali Shayesteh,Fadwa El Mellouhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍RAINBOT™，一种基于消费级3D打印机改造的低成本、开源可复现的液体处理机器人。通过将打印机挤出头替换为精密单通道移液器，并集成Python控制的线性执行器实现柱塞和吸头弹出动作。系统配备基于浏览器的数字孪生系统，实现与物理平台双向同步，支持远程监控和紧急停止。作为概念验证，RAINBOT™成功执行了有色水溶液的顺序交换实验，色传感器测量的RYB响应与预期混合行为平均绝对误差仅2个百分点。该系统与CEID™框架耦合，将实验从迭代手动猜测转变为目标导向的逆设计搜索。总硬件成本低于1300美元，约为入门级商业系统的十分之一，为自驱动实验室自动化建立了可访问的物理-虚拟框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业液体处理系统成本高昂、专有设计且远程监督能力有限，严重制约了实验室自动化的普及。需要一种低成本、开源可复现且支持远程监督的替代方案，以降低自动化实验的门槛。

Method: 将消费级Cartesian 3D打印机(Elegoo Neptune 4 Max)的挤出头替换为精密单通道移液器，利用打印机原有的G-code驱动X-Y-Z龙门架实现定位，通过两个紧凑型线性执行器在Python控制下实现柱塞运动和吸头弹出。构建基于浏览器的数字孪生系统，实现与物理平台的双向实时同步，包括运动学和移液状态，支持远程监控、干预和急停。与CEID™框架耦合实现逆设计搜索。

Result: 在有色水溶液顺序交换实验中，集成颜色传感器测量的红、黄、蓝(RYB)响应与预期混合行为的平均绝对误差仅为2个百分点，验证了执行正确性和实时追踪能力。总硬件成本低于1300美元，约为入门级商业系统的十分之一。

Conclusion: RAINBOT™成功展示了低成本、开源可复现的液体处理机器人的可行性，通过数字孪生实现远程监督，并通过与CEID™框架耦合实现目标导向的实验自动化。该工作为自驱动实验室自动化建立了易于获取的物理-虚拟框架，有望加速实验室自动化的广泛应用。

Abstract: Laboratory automation accelerates discovery, yet its adoption is constrained by the high cost, proprietary design, and limited remote supervisability of commercial liquid-handling systems. This work presents RAINBOT\textsuperscript{TM}, a low-cost, openly reproducible liquid-handling robot built by converting a consumer-grade Cartesian 3D printer (Elegoo Neptune 4 Max). The printer extruder is replaced by a precision single-channel pipette actuated through the printer's own G-code-driven X--Y--Z gantry, with plunger and tip-eject motions effected by two compact linear actuators under Python control. To make experiments transparent and remotely supervisable, a browser-based digital twin is implemented to synchronise bidirectionally with the physical platform, mirroring kinematics and pipetting states in real time and exposing remote monitoring, intervention, and an emergency stop from any web browser. As a proof of concept, RAINBOT\textsuperscript{TM} performed sequential exchanges of differently coloured aqueous solutions while an integrated colour sensor quantified the resulting mixtures; measured red, yellow, and blue (RYB) responses agreed with expected mixing behaviour to within a mean absolute error of two percentage points, validating correct execution and real-time tracking. Closing the loop, the platform is coupled to the CEID\textsuperscript{TM} (Cooperative Explorer for Inverse Design) framework, which recasts experimentation from iterative manual guessing into a goal-directed inverse-design search while keeping a human in the loop. The complete hardware costs under US\$1300, which is roughly an order of magnitude below entry-level commercial handlers, thereby establishing an accessible physical--virtual framework for self-driving laboratory automation.

</details>


### [334] [Safe and Scalable Multi-Drone Payload Transport via CBF-based Reinforcement Learning with Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20665)
*Jaeyoun Choi,Oswin So,Songyuan Zhang,Cooper Taylor,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习的框架DGPPO，用于多无人机协同载荷运输。通过最小2D抽象保持无人机-缆绳-载荷耦合动力学，结合域随机化和离散图控制屏障函数PPO算法，实现了零样本sim-to-real迁移。真实世界实验证明单一策略可泛化到不同团队规模和动态环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机载荷运输在建筑、物流和灾难响应中有广阔应用前景，但无人机-缆绳-载荷之间的复杂耦合动力学给安全性和可扩展性带来了巨大挑战，现有方法在动态非结构化环境中表现有限。

Method: 提出最小2D抽象模型保留任务相关的耦合动力学；使用域随机化覆盖团队规模和物理参数；提出离散图控制屏障函数PPO（DGPPO）训练完全分布式策略，实现零样本sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 单一学习策略可泛化到不同团队规模和任务场景；多组硬件实验表明同一策略能在其他无人机团队作为移动障碍物的动态环境中安全运行。

Conclusion: 所提框架实现了高效、安全、可扩展的多无人机载荷运输，在复杂真实世界条件下具有强泛化能力。

Abstract: Multi-drone payload transportation has emerged as a promising research paradigm with potential applications in construction, logistics, and disaster response. However, the complex coupled dynamics among drones, cables, and payloads pose significant challenges, and existing approaches remain limited in safety and scalability, particularly in dynamic and unstructured environments. In this work, we propose a learning-based framework for safe and scalable multi-drone cooperative payload transport. We introduce a minimal 2D abstraction that preserves the task-relevant drone-payload coupling required for coordination and safety, while remaining computationally efficient for large-scale learning. Using domain randomization over team size and physical parameters, we train a fully distributed policy via Discrete Graph Control Barrier Function Proximal Policy Optimization (DGPPO), enabling robust zero-shot sim-to-real transfer without fine-tuning. Extensive real-world evaluations demonstrate that a single learned policy generalizes across varying team sizes and task scenarios. Furthermore, multi-group hardware experiments show that the same policy can safely operate in dynamic environments, where other drone teams act as moving obstacles. These results indicate that the proposed framework enables efficient, safe, and scalable multi-drone payload transportation with strong generalization to complex real-world conditions.

</details>


### [335] [Towards Capability-Aware Traversability Navigation for Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20679)
*Gianluca Capezzuto,Felipe Tommaselli,Matheus P. Angarola,Ricardo V. Godoy,Marcelo Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Capability-Aware Traversability (CAT)框架，通过将机器人物理能力约束直接嵌入空间特征空间，实现面向不同形态机器人的可通行性估计。CAT利用交互式标注管道从物理轨迹生成密集监督掩码，并通过Spatially-Adaptive Denormalization (SPADE)模块将机器人特有的可通行性向量调制到语义地形图中。在人工标注和轨迹对齐数据集上，CAT在所有排序指标上领先，AUROC提升11.0%，AUPRC提升15.8%。消融实验表明空间条件化和每机器人原型产生超出通用路径预测的能力敏感性。在四足机器人和轮式滑移转向机器人上的部署展示了在嵌入式硬件上以4.8Hz运行的具身感知避障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可通行性估计方法往往通过后期轨迹过滤来跨形态迁移预测，而非将平台约束编码到学习表示中，导致不同形态机器人对同一地形的可通行性判断不准确。

Method: 提出CAT框架：(1) 通过交互式标注管道从物理轨迹生成密集监督掩码；(2) 使用Spatially-Adaptive Denormalization (SPADE)模块将机器人特有的可通行性向量调制到语义地形特征中，将物理能力约束直接嵌入空间特征空间。

Result: 在人工标注和轨迹对齐数据集上，CAT在所有排序指标上领先，AUROC提升11.0%，AUPRC提升15.8%。在四足机器人和轮式机器人上以4.8Hz运行，实现具身感知避障。

Conclusion: 将机器人物理能力约束直接编码到空间特征表示中，比后期轨迹过滤更能有效实现跨形态的可通行性估计，且具备实时部署能力。

Abstract: Estimating traversability in unstructured environments requires conditioning on robot embodiment, as the same terrain can be traversable for one platform and unsafe for another. Existing methods often transfer predictions across morphologies through late-stage trajectory filtering rather than encoding platform constraints in the learned representation. We propose Capability-Aware Traversability (CAT), a framework that embeds physical limits directly into the spatial feature space. CAT grounds dense supervision masks in physical trajectories through an interactive annotation pipeline and modulates semantic terrain maps with robot-specific traversability vectors through Spatially-Adaptive Denormalization (SPADE) blocks. Across human-annotated and trajectory-aligned datasets, CAT leads all ranking-based metrics, improving AUROC by 11.0% on physically executed trajectories and AUPRC by 15.8% on human traces over the strongest baseline. Ablations show that spatial conditioning and per-robot prototypes produce capability sensitivity beyond generic path prediction. Deployments on a legged quadruped and a wheeled skid-steer demonstrate embodiment-aware obstacle avoidance on embedded hardware at 4.8 Hz.

</details>


### [336] [FELT: Generating Tactile Signals from Vision for Visuo-Tactile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20683)
*Zinan Li,Yiyang Ling,Yuming Gu,Binghao Huang,Chenhao Liang,Sharfin Islam,Hisham Bedri,John Chirikjian,Yunzhu Li,Stefanos Nikolaidis,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FELT是一个能从RGB观测中合成每指压力触觉图像的框架，通过冻结的视觉编码器和轻量查询解码器预测触觉信号，并在四类接触丰富的操作任务中证明其能提升策略成功率，减少对真实触觉传感器数据的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉对于操作任务至关重要，但触觉传感器数据比视觉数据稀缺，因为触觉传感器易损、专用且难以标准化。为解决触觉数据不足的问题，提出从RGB观测合成触觉信号的方法。

Method: 提出FELT框架，使用大型冻结视觉编码器+轻量查询解码器，通过单次前向传播预测触觉信号；针对双指触觉传感器物理拓扑，分左右分支解码触觉面板以捕捉非对称接触模式。

Result: 在擦拭、插入、手内旋转等四类接触丰富操作任务上，生成的触觉图像和潜在触觉特征均优于仅用视觉的基线策略；其中潜在触觉特征在策略训练和部署中无需真实触觉传感器。

Conclusion: FELT能够有效利用视觉数据合成触觉信号，减少对真实触觉数据的依赖，提升接触丰富操作任务的成功率，且潜在触觉特征模式可实现无真实触觉传感器的策略学习。

Abstract: The sense of touch is central to manipulation, especially when vision is occluded or ambiguous. Although combining vision and touch improves manipulation, learning robust visuo-tactile policies requires substantial tactile data. Such data remains scarcer than visual data, because tactile sensors are fragile, specialized, and hard to standardize. To address this, we present Feature-Extracted Latent Tactile (FELT), a learning-based framework that synthesizes per-finger pressure tactile images from RGB observations, reducing the need for tactile-equipped data collection. FELT uses a large frozen visual encoder and a lightweight query decoder to predict tactile signals in a single feed-forward pass. To respect the physical topology of dual-finger tactile sensors, FELT decodes the left and right tactile sensor panels through separate branches, capturing the asymmetric contact patterns during interactions such as wiping, insertion, and in-hand rotation. At inference time, FELT only requires RGB data, allowing us to augment existing vision-only data with tactile observations, either as generated tactile images or as latent tactile features. Experiments on four contact-rich manipulation tasks demonstrate that both generated tactile images and latent tactile features improve policy success over vision-only baselines, with latent feature requiring no real tactile sensor during policy training or deployment. Supplementary material is available on our anonymous website: https://felt-tactile.github.io/.

</details>


### [337] [Decentralized UAV Swarms for Ground Target Protection in GPS- and Communication-Denied Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20710)
*Dimitria Silveria,Paulo Ricardo Marques de Araujo,Tiago Nascimento,Sidney Givigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在通信和GPS受限环境下，利用自主无人机群保护地面目标免受敌方无人机攻击的管道方法，通过卡尔曼滤波估计目标状态，并采用去中心化编队包围技术适应目标运动，使用真实机器人验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机防御方法大多依赖无人机间通信和全球定位系统，但在现代战争场景中这些资源可能不可用，因此需要开发不依赖通信和GPS的自主无人机群防御方案。

Method: 1) 假设通信和GPS拒绝环境，无人机仅使用机载传感器进行目标跟踪和群体协调；2) 开发卡尔曼滤波器，利用相对测量值估计未知目标状态和无人机位置；3) 提出去中心化群体包围技术，通过包围目标最大化覆盖范围，并能自适应目标运动。

Result: 使用真实机器人进行广泛验证，结果表明该方法在检测、包围和拦截敌方无人机方面具有有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够在通信和GPS受限条件下，通过自主无人机群有效保护地面目标免受无人机攻击，为现代战争场景提供了可行的反制方案。

Abstract: The presence of UAVs in military operations has recently increased, also increasing the demand for defense systems against UAV attacks. UAVs can also be used as countermeasures. Most available methods rely on UAV-to-UAV communication and global positioning. However, such resources may not be available in modern warfare scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose a pipeline for ground-target protection against UAV attacks that employs autonomous swarms of UAVs. We assume a communication- and GPS-denied environment in which the UAVs use onboard sensors to track the target and coordinate as a swarm. We developed Kalman filters to estimate the states of unknown targets and the positions of UAVs in the swarm using only relative measurements. Also, our strategy is to encircle the target of interest to maximize coverage. To achieve that, we propose a decentralized swarm encirclement technique that adapts to the target's motion. Our approach was extensively validated using real robots, demonstrating its effectiveness in detecting, encircling, and intercepting hostile UAVs.

</details>


### [338] [Self-Supervised Bio-Inspired Robotic Trajectory Planning with Obstacle Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20743)
*Miroslav Krupa,Miroslav Cibula,Kristína Malinovská*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受神经科学启发的自监督学习框架用于机器人轨迹规划，利用前向和逆向模型作为内部监督机制。实验证明了可行性，但发现规划器会利用学习信号产生漏洞，为此提出了额外的训练策略和缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于采样的轨迹规划方法在高维空间和障碍物密集环境中计算成本高；基于模型学习的方法虽然高效，但存在样本效率低或泛化能力有限的问题。因此，需要一种无需专家演示且样本高效的学习方法。

Method: 采用神经启发的自监督学习框架，利用前向模型（预测状态转移）和逆向模型（预测动作）作为内部监督信号，在含障碍物的环境中训练轨迹规划器，使规划器通过有限次前向传播即可生成轨迹。

Result: 实验验证了该方法的可行性，但发现规划器会利用前向和逆向模型提供的学习信号进行"欺骗性"学习（exploit），导致规划质量下降。为此，作者提出并评估了额外的训练制度和缓解策略。

Conclusion: 自监督学习框架在轨迹规划中具有潜力，但需要谨慎设计训练机制以防止规划器利用内部监督信号中的漏洞，未来需进一步优化缓解策略以提升鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Trajectory planning is a fundamental problem in robotics, requiring the generation of collision-free and efficient trajectories in a potentially complex environment. While sampling-based planners remain the dominant approach, they are often computationally expensive, particularly in high-dimensional spaces and obstacle-rich environments. Methods based on model learning offer a promising alternative, enabling efficient planning through a bounded number of forward passes through a neural trajectory planner, but commonly suffer from low sample efficiency or limited generalisation due to their reliance on exploration or expert demonstrations. This follow-up work tests our neuro-inspired self-supervised learning framework for trajectory planning that leverages forward and inverse models as the internal supervisory mechanism in an environment that contains an obstacle. Experimental results demonstrate the feasibility of the approach while revealing a tendency of our planner to exploit the learning signal provided by the forward and inverse models. To address this issue, additional training regimes and mitigation strategies are proposed and evaluated.

</details>


### [339] [A real-time RGB-D perception pipeline for autonomous impact hammers in mining: self-filtering, rock segmentation and rock-breaking poses generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20748)
*Martín Gallegos,Francisco Leiva,Patricio Loncomilla,Michelle Cortés,Javier Ruiz-del-Solar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于RGB-D感知的实时视觉管线，用于自动化矿山液压冲击锤操作，能够同时生成可行的破碎位姿和无机器人工作空间3D表示，在嵌入式硬件上以约10Hz（延迟675ms）运行，实验验证了其在真实场景中的实时自治操作可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前矿山中的冲击锤（碎石机）多依赖远程遥控操作，效率低下，亟需自动化解决方案以提高作业效率和安全性。

Method: 结合基于图像的实例分割与几何点云处理，在嵌入式硬件上构建RGB-D感知管线，实时输出可操作的破碎位姿和无机器人工作空间3D表示。

Result: 系统在代表性缩尺场景中验证有效，运行频率约10Hz，总延迟约675ms，满足实时闭环控制需求。

Conclusion: 该感知管线适用于冲击锤的实时自主操作，为矿山自动化提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Impact hammers, also known as rock-breakers, are essential machines in mining operations, where they perform secondary reduction. In underground mining, these machines are typically teleoperated, limiting operational efficiency. This paper presents a real-time RGB-D perception pipeline as a step towards automating the operation of hydraulic impact hammers used in mining. The proposed system simultaneously generates operationally feasible rock-breaking poses and a robot-free 3D representation of the workspace. The proposed approach combines image-based instance segmentation with geometric point cloud processing, and operates on embedded hardware at approximately 10 Hz with a total latency of around 675 ms, enabling responsive closed-loop behavior when integrated with a control system. Experimental results in a representative scaled scenario demonstrate that the proposed system is suitable for real-time autonomous impact hammer operation.

</details>


### [340] [Emergent Compositional Skills in Mixture-of-Experts VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20771)
*Shlok Shah,Rhiaan Jhaveri,Tharun Kumar Tiruppali Kalidoss,Chirayu Nimonkar,Ishaan Javali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用混合专家（MoE）动作头训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，使其能够从专家演示中端到端学习组合式机器人策略，无需预先定义任务分解或层级结构。实验表明，学习到的专家单元被跨任务重用，且对应于不同质的低级行为，路由器隐式学习了高层任务序列化，专家则充当可组合的原语。MoE在保持任务性能的同时展现了有意义的专家专业化，实现了从数据中涌现模块化、可解释的机器人策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略通常需要预定义的任务分解或层级结构来学习组合式行为，作者希望探索是否可以通过端到端学习让模型自主发现任务的组合结构，从而无需人工指定模块划分即可获得可重用、可解释的策略原语。

Method: 在标准VLA模型基础上，将原本单一的动作预测头替换为简化的混合专家（MoE）动作头，包含多个专家网络和一个路由网络。路由器根据输入的视觉-语言特征动态选择激活哪些专家来生成动作，整个模型通过端到端方式在专家演示数据上训练，无需任何任务分解的监督信号。

Result: MoE模型在任务性能上与单一网络基线持平；学习到的专家被不同任务高度重用，且不同专家展现出定性上可区分的低级行为模式；路由器在不同任务上下文中选择了不同的专家组合，表明其隐式学会了高层任务序列化。

Conclusion: MoE架构作为一种让机器人策略从数据中自然涌现模块化、可解释结构的有效途径，无需人工先验即可实现组合式行为学习，为构建可解释、可迁移的机器人策略提供了新方向。

Abstract: We consider the problem of learning compositional robot policies end-to-end from expert demonstrations, without any pre-specified notion of task decomposition or hierarchy. We ask whether a VLA trained with a simplified Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) action head can emergently learn to decompose tasks into reusable, interpretable primitives. We find that learned experts are heavily reused across tasks and consistently correspond to qualitatively distinct low-level behaviors, suggesting that the router implicitly learns to perform high-level sequencing while experts serve as compositional primitives. Our MoE matches the task performance of a monolithic baseline while demonstrating meaningful expert specialization, a step toward modular, interpretable robot policies that emerge from data alone.

</details>


### [341] [Socially Consistent Multi-Robot Navigation Using Decoupled Planning and Trajectory Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20772)
*Matthew M. Sato,Kincho H. Law*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种部分去中心化的框架，通过解耦全局路径规划与轨迹协调，生成可预测且符合社会规范的多机器人运动。具体包括：1) 改进的A*规划器，将宏观社会规范嵌入成本函数；2) 共享路径构建社会图以增强路径一致性；3) 将多机器人轨迹协调问题建模为混合整数凸规划，高效计算无冲突轨迹。实验表明，在路径规划阶段强制执行社会一致性可产生可预测且符合社会规范的路径，并简化多机器人协调问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有短期人感知规划方法缺乏确保长期一致性和可预测行为的机制，导致局部规划器负担过重，产生低效且局部反应性的运动，降低了可预测性。

Method: 1) 改进A*规划器，将宏观社会规范嵌入成本函数；2) 移动机器人间共享路径，协同构建已建立路线的社会图，强制路径一致性并减少未来规划开销；3) 利用社会约束路径的涌现结构，将多机器人轨迹协调问题表述为混合整数凸规划，支持高效计算和大规模扩展。

Result: 在路径规划阶段强制执行社会一致性能够产生可预测、符合社会规范的移动机器人路径，并简化多机器人协调这一复杂问题。

Conclusion: 社会一致性约束应提前在路径规划阶段引入，而非仅依赖局部规划器，这有助于提升长期行为的可预测性和社会合规性，并有效简化多机器人轨迹协调的计算复杂性。

Abstract: The successful integration of mobile robots in human-centric environments requires navigation that is not only safe and efficient, but also predictable and aligned with social conventions, key precursors for human comfort and acceptance. While significant research addresses short-term human-aware planning, these methods often lack mechanisms for ensuring consistent and predictable behaviors across long horizons. Without socially aware long-term planners, local planners are overburdened, resulting in inefficient and locally reactive movements that undermine predictability. This paper introduces a partially decentralized framework that generates predictable and socially consistent multi-robot motion by decoupling global path planning from trajectory coordination. First, we propose a modified A* planner that embeds macroscopic social norms into the planner cost function. Planned paths are shared across mobile robots to collaboratively build a social graph of established routes, which enforces path consistency and reduces future planning effort. Second, we leverage the emergent structure of the socially constrained paths to formulate the multi-robot trajectory coordination problem as a mixed-integer convex program. The convex program enables efficient computation of conflict- free trajectories, scaling effectively to large fleets and supporting dynamic task assignment. Our results demonstrate that enforcing social consistency at the path planning stage produces predictable, socially compliant mobile robot paths and simplifies the otherwise complex problem of multi-robot coordination.

</details>


### [342] [Robostral Navigate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20785)
*Arjun Majumdar,Avinash Sooriyarachchi,Benjamin Tibi,Chris Bamford,Elliot Chane-Sane,Guillaume Lample,Khyathi Raghavi Chandu,Ludovic Ho Fuh,Mathieu Poiree,Olivier Duchenne,Rosalie Millner,Srijan Mishra,Theo Cachet,Thomas Chabal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Robostral Navigate，一个8B视觉语言模型，仅使用单目RGB图像进行导航，通过指向图像空间中的目标位置预测航点。模型在350k模拟场景中生成240万条轨迹，采用前缀缓存训练策略（减少22倍训练token）和树状注意力掩码，结合强化学习优化探索与恢复能力。在R2R-CE和RxR-CE基准上达到SOTA，分别实现77.4%和75.1%的成功率，超越依赖深度传感器或多相机系统的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模部署导航系统需要最小化传感器假设、跨机器人形态泛化及高效训练，而现有系统依赖深度传感器、多相机或预建地图，硬件限制大、部署成本高。为此，本文旨在构建仅需单目RGB这一最普适传感器的导航模型。

Method: 1) 构建8B视觉语言模型，仅输入单目RGB图像，在图像空间（而非机器人坐标系）预测指向下一目标的航点，天然鲁棒于相机内参和场景尺度变化。2) 在350k模拟场景中生成240万条轨迹，降低对真实数据采集的依赖。3) 提出前缀缓存训练策略：将完整episode打包为单个训练序列，训练token减少22倍，训练时间从月缩短至天。4) 树状注意力掩码防止模型依赖历史真值动作，鼓励基于视觉的动作预测。5) 使用强化学习进一步提升探索和恢复能力。

Result: R2R-CE基准上成功率达77.4%，超越最优单目方法10.5个百分点，超越依赖深度或多相机的最强系统5.3个百分点。RxR-CE基准上成功率达75.1%，超越所有单目基线。

Conclusion: 仅用单目RGB即可达到甚至超越依赖深度传感器或多相机系统的导航性能，验证了纯视觉导航在大规模跨形态部署中的可行性和高效性。

Abstract: Deploying navigation systems at scale requires a recipe that minimizes sensor assumptions, generalizes across robot embodiments, and trains efficiently. Yet, today's best systems depend on depth sensors, multi-camera rigs, or pre-built maps, limiting the hardware they support and increasing deployment cost. We introduce Robostral Navigate, an 8B vision-language model built around this scalability objective. The model consumes only a stream of monocular RGB images - the most ubiquitous sensor across robotic platforms and predicts waypoints by pointing to the next target location in the current camera view. Operating purely in image space, rather than robot-specific coordinates, makes the policy naturally robust to changes in camera intrinsics and scene scale, enabling deployment across wheeled, legged, and aerial robots without recalibration. We generate 2.4 million trajectories across 350k simulated scenes to reduce the reliance on real-world data collection and scale easily. We further introduce a prefix-caching training recipe that packs entire episodes into single training sequences, reducing training tokens by 22x and cutting training time from months to days. A tree-based attention mask prevents conditioning on previous ground-truth actions, encouraging visually grounded action prediction, and reinforcement learning is used to further improve exploration and recovery capabilities. On the Room-to-Room and Room-Across-Room in Continuous Environments (R2R-CE and RxR-CE) benchmarks, Robostral Navigate sets a new state of the art. On R2R-CE, it achieves a 77.4% success rate, surpassing the best monocular method by 10.5 points and the strongest depth- or multi-camera system by 5.3 points despite using only a single RGB camera. On RxR-CE, it reaches 75.1% success rate, outperforming all monocular baselines.

</details>


### [343] [URF: A Unified Robot Control-Policy Framework for Stable Contact Aware Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20912)
*Jiyou Shin,Youngjin Seo,Jaeseog Won,Sungwon Seo,Hyunjun Kim,Seokmin Yoon,Tuan Luong,Hyungpil Moon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: URF提出统一阻抗-导纳控制框架，让机器人策略不仅预测虚拟目标和刚度矩阵，还预测阻抗-导纳切换比率，以在刚性接触中兼顾运动跟踪精度和安全接触。通过接触力构建切换比率标签进行监督，提升了任务成功率并减少了导纳控制常见的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在刚性接触任务中，传统的"感知→动作→底层控制"分离范式存在问题：同样的动作指令在不同底层控制器下会导致不稳定接触、跟踪误差、过载或工具损坏，因为底层控制器的行为未与任务需求协同优化。

Method: 提出URF框架，从多模态观测中同时预测三个量：虚拟目标（virtual target）、刚度矩阵（stiffness matrix）、阻抗-导纳切换比率（impedance-admittance switch ratio）。切换比率决定控制器何时偏向导纳控制（精确运动跟踪）或阻抗控制（安全刚性接触）。由于演示数据缺乏环境刚度真值，利用实测接触力构造切换比率标签来监督控制器模式预测。

Result: 在翻箱（box-flipping）和压线（line-pressing）任务中，URF相比纯导纳执行实现了更高的任务成功率，并减少了快速力累积、大幅力振荡、工具断裂和机器人安全停止等失效模式。

Conclusion: 接触感知策略不仅应预测 compliant 动作，还应预测用于执行这些动作的控制器行为（即控制器模式切换），这有助于提升刚性接触任务的安全性和成功率。

Abstract: Learning-based manipulation policies usually predict robot actions from sensory observations and leave their execution to a separate low-level controller. In rigid contact, this separation can be problematic: the same motion to a virtual target or compliant motion command can lead to unstable contact, tracking error, excessive loading, or tool damage, depending on the low-level controller. In this paper, we propose a \textit{Unified Robot Control-Policy Framework} (URF), which connects compliant action prediction with unified impedance-admittance control. Given multimodal observations, URF predicts a virtual target, a stiffness matrix, and an impedance-admittance switch ratio. The switch ratio determines when the controller should behave more like admittance control for accurate motion tracking and when it should move toward impedance control for safer rigid contact. Because demonstration data do not provide ground-truth environment stiffness, we construct switch-ratio labels from measured contact forces and use them to supervise controller-mode prediction. Across box-flipping and line-pressing tasks, URF achieves higher task success rates while reducing failure modes observed with admittance-only execution, including rapid force buildup, large force oscillations, tool breakage, and robot safety stops. These results suggest that contact-aware policies benefit from predicting not only compliant actions but also the controller behavior used to execute them. Project page: https://jiyou384.github.io/urf_project_page/

</details>


### [344] [Deep Reinforcement-Learning-Guided Model Predictive Control for Preventing Overtakes in Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20973)
*Yufei Xi,Yijie Liao,Tulga Ersal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层强化学习引导的模型预测控制框架，将防守性阻挡问题建模为空间占据调节问题。通过在Frenet域中运行Soft Actor-Critic策略层生成几何感知的防守参考，并将其作为空间正则化嵌入非线性模型预测控制中。在Thunderhill West赛道仿真中，平均超车时间从8.8秒提升至14.6秒，对手进展显著降低，同时车辆利用83.4%的可用轮胎力，平均求解时间33.3毫秒，支持实时高速对抗交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶赛车中，防守性阻挡（防止更快对手超车）不同于单纯的最小化圈时，需要车辆在动态极限附近运行时主动占据空间位置以阻挡对手。现有方法缺乏针对防守策略的专门建模，因此需要一种能够在高速动态环境下生成几何感知防守行为的方法。

Method: 将防守问题形式化为空间占据调节问题；采用分层框架：上层为Frenet域中的Soft Actor-Critic强化学习策略层，生成几何感知的防守参考轨迹；下层为非线性模型预测控制（NMPC），将防守参考作为空间正则化项嵌入，并在摩擦力约束下进行优化控制。

Result: 在Thunderhill West赛道仿真中，平均超车时间从8.8秒增加到14.6秒；对手进展显著降低；车辆利用83.4%的可用轮胎力；平均求解时间33.3毫秒（标准差13.9毫秒），满足实时性要求。

Conclusion: 该分层强化学习-MPC框架能有效将防守超车时间提升约66%，同时保持高轮胎力利用率和实时计算可行性，为自动驾驶赛车中的防御性驾驶策略提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses defensive blocking in autonomous racing, where a vehicle must prevent a faster opponent from overtaking while operating near its dynamic limits. Different from lap-time minimization, we formulate defense as a spatial occupancy regulation problem via a hierarchical reinforcement-learning guided model predictive control framework. A Soft Actor-Critic strategic layer operates in the Frenet domain to generate geometry-aware defensive references, which are embedded into the nonlinear model predictive control formulation as spatial regularization under friction constraints. Evaluated on the Thunderhill West circuit in simulation, the framework increases average overtake time from 8.8 s to 14.6 s while significantly reducing opponent progress. Meanwhile, it allows the vehicle to utilize 83.4% of available tire force. The framework achieves a 33.3 ms mean solve time (13.9 ms std), supporting real-time high-speed adversarial interaction.

</details>


### [345] [Distributed Model-Based Diffusion For Scalable Multi-Robot Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20992)
*Haejoon Lee,Xinyi Wang,Taekyung Kim,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分布式模型驱动扩散（DMBD）框架，通过将逆向扩散过程分解为局部条件逆向扩散过程，实现多机器人系统的高效分布式轨迹优化，在多种场景下实现亚秒级求解并显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型驱动扩散（MBD）方法扩展到多机器人系统时存在中心化高维推理问题，面临维度诅咒导致的低样本效率，且需要全局访问所有机器人的动力学、约束和目标。

Method: 提出DMBD分布式服务器-机器人框架，将逆向扩散过程分解为各机器人独立在其控制子空间内进行去噪的局部条件逆向扩散过程，通过服务器聚合和广播其他机器人的当前轨迹估计作为条件。

Result: 在目标交换、多层覆盖、停车和高峰交通等多种场景中的大量仿真表明，DMBD具有强可扩展性，能在亚秒级解决许多具有挑战性的协调任务，显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: DMBD成功解决了多机器人轨迹优化中中心化高维推理的瓶颈，实现了分布式高效协调，为多机器人系统在复杂环境下的实时轨迹优化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Trajectory optimization for multi-robot systems remains a critical challenge, particularly when navigating highly non-convex, non-linear, and non-differentiable environments. While Model-Based Diffusion (MBD) has recently emerged as a promising sampling-based optimization paradigm for single-robot trajectory generation, extending it to multi-robot systems results in a centralized, high-dimensional inference problem that (i) suffers from poor sample efficiency due to the curse of dimensionality and (ii) requires global access to all robots' dynamics, constraints, and objectives. To address this, we propose Distributed Model-Based Diffusion (DMBD), a distributed server-robot framework that decomposes the reverse diffusion process into local conditional reverse diffusion processes. This decomposition enables each robot to iteratively perform denoising independently within its own control subspace while conditioning on the current trajectory estimates of the other robots that are aggregated and broadcast by the server. Extensive simulations in goal swapping, multi-floor coverage, parking, and rush-hour scenarios demonstrate that DMBD achieves strong scalability, solving many challenging coordination tasks in sub-seconds and significantly outperforming existing baselines.

</details>


### [346] [TableVerse: A Large-scale Tabletop Dataset with Real-world Grounded Layouts for Generalizable Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21017)
*Boyuan Wang,Yue Zhang,Xutao Xue,Xueyu Song,Yu Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TableVerse 提出了一种全自动 Real2Sim 流水线，从无结构的互联网图像数据直接重建高保真、物理一致的桌面环境，并自动生成碰撞避免的抓取-放置轨迹，构建了包含10万个独特环境及交互轨迹的 TableVerse-100K 数据集，为通用机器人操作策略提供大规模、高保真的数据基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化合成方法（如文本到布局生成或简化的程序化生成）生成的场景往往物理上不合理，且无法模拟真实人类环境中复杂密集的杂乱状态，制约了通用机器人操作策略的泛化能力。

Method: 提出 TableVerse 全自动 Real2Sim 流水线，直接从无结构的互联网图像数据中确定性地重建高保真桌面环境，包括精确公制尺度、真实拓扑结构和机械稳定性验证；并集成自动化任务条件轨迹生成框架，合成无碰撞的抓取-放置演示。

Result: 构建了 TableVerse-100K 数据集，包含100,000个独特的、物理一致的桌面环境及其交互操作轨迹，场景具有多样的资产组合、真实的空间分布和高品质的演示数据。

Conclusion: TableVerse-100K 提供了高度可扩展且高保真的数据基础，对推动通用机器人操作任务的未来研究具有重要价值。

Abstract: The development of generalizable robotic manipulation policies is inherently bounded by the availability of large-scale, high-fidelity scene data. While recent automated synthesis methods attempt to bridge this gap via text-to-layout hallucination or simplified procedural generation, they frequently suffer from physical implausibility and fail to capture the complex, dense clutter of actual human environments. In this paper, we introduce TableVerse, a fully automated Real2Sim pipeline that shifts the paradigm from imaginative layout generation to deterministic reconstruction from unstructured, in-the-wild image data. Our framework seamlessly processes unscripted internet media into high-fidelity, simulation-ready tabletop environments with accurate metric scales, authentic topologies, and verified mechanical stability. Furthermore, an automated task-conditioned trajectory generation framework is integrated to synthesize high-quality, collision-free pick-and-place demonstrations. Leveraging this complete pipeline, we construct the TableVerse-100K Dataset, a large-scale corpus comprising 100,000 unique, physically consistent environments paired with interactive manipulation trajectories. By capturing diverse asset compositions, realistic spatial distributions, and high-quality demonstrations, TableVerse-100K establishes a highly scalable and high-fidelity data foundation, providing significant value to facilitate future research in generalizable robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [347] [ZONDA: Zero-shot Object Navigation with Dynamic Avoidance in Multi-floor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21025)
*Shaomin Liang,Xuanhong Liao,Shiyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZONDA是一种零样本（zero-shot）目标导航框架，通过启发式多楼层规划、多视图目标验证和动态行人避让三大模块，在真实双足机器人及HM3D/MP3D仿真环境中实现跨楼层、动态场景下的鲁棒导航，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标导航方法受限于静态、单楼层环境，忽略跨楼层拓扑结构和动态行人，难以在真实世界部署。

Method: 提出ZONDA框架，包含三个核心组件：(i) 启发式多楼层规划——基于高度差可通行地图实现楼梯穿越和跨楼层探索，无需平台特定的学习控制器；(ii) 多视图目标验证——结合视觉-语言模型对多尺度观测进行交叉验证，显著降低假阳性；(iii) 动态行人避让——显式跟踪并预测移动行人以产生预期性避让行为。

Result: 在真实Direct Drive Tech TITA双足机器人及HM3D/MP3D仿真上取得显著改进，在动态基准HM3D-DYNA上保持鲁棒导航，优于现有基线。

Conclusion: ZONDA通过零样本方式有效解决了跨楼层规划和动态行人避让两大关键挑战，提升了目标导航在真实复杂环境中的部署能力。

Abstract: In Object Goal Navigation task, existing methods are typically restricted to static and single-floor environments, ignoring cross-floor topologies and dynamic pedestrian, which limits their real-world deployment. To address these limitations, we propose ZONDA, a zero-shot object navigation with dynamic avoidance framework. In particular, ZONDA integrates three core components: (i) Heuristic multi-floor planning: from height-difference traversable maps, enables stair traversal and cross-floor exploration without a platform-specific learned controller; (ii) Multi-view target verification: cross-checks multi-scale observations with a vision-language model, significantly reducing false positives; and (iii) Dynamic pedestrian avoidance: explicitly tracks and predicts moving pedestrians to generate anticipatory behaviors. Evaluated on a real Direct Drive Tech TITA biped robot and extensive simulations on HM3D and MP3D, ZONDA achieves significantly improved results. Moreover, ZONDA can maintain robust navigation on the dynamic benchmark HM3D-DYNA compared to the existing baseline.

</details>


### [348] [A Real-Time Generalized Nash Equilibrium Framework for Interaction-Aware Autonomous Driving in Mixed Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21043)
*Nouhed Naidja,Mohamed-Cherif Rahal,Steve Pechberti,Stéphane Font,Guillaume Sandou,Marc Revilloud*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义纳什均衡问题（GNEP）的自动驾驶决策框架，显式建模了自车与人类驾驶者之间的共享安全与几何约束，并采用粒子群优化（PSO）求解器进行实时求解。在真实测试赛道上的自动驾驶Renault Zoé与人类驾驶者交互实验验证了该方法能够生成舒适、类人的轨迹，求解器收敛时间低于50毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合交通环境中，自动驾驶车辆与人类驾驶者之间存在复杂的相互依赖关系，传统的解耦优化方法未能有效建模这种策略与对手行为之间的动态耦合，导致安全与通行效率难以兼顾。

Method: 将驾驶交互建模为广义纳什均衡问题（GNEP），显式引入共享安全约束与几何约束，使自车策略的可行性动态依赖于对手行为；采用粒子群优化（PSO）求解该非凸问题以实现实时求解。

Result: 在真实测试赛道上使用自动驾驶Renault Zoé与人类驾驶者进行实验，系统能够处理关键场景并生成舒适、类人的轨迹；PSO求解器在50毫秒内收敛，验证了计算可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的GNEP框架能有效建模AV与人类驾驶者之间的相互依赖关系，PSO求解器满足实时性要求，在真实环境中展现出安全、舒适的类人驾驶能力。

Abstract: Safe and efficient navigation in mixed-traffic environments remains a critical challenge for Autonomous Vehicles (AVs), primarily due to the complex interdependence between the AV's decisions and the unpredictable reactions of human drivers. This paper introduces a comprehensive decision-making framework that formulates the driving interaction as a Generalized Nash Equilibrium Problem (GNEP). Unlike decoupled optimization approaches, this framework explicitly models shared safety and geometric constraints, ensuring that the feasibility of the AV's strategy is dynamically linked to the opponent's actions. To solve this non-convex problem in real-time, we propose a dedicated solver based on Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO). The complete architecture was validated on a test track using a real autonomous Renault Zoé interacting with a human driver. Experimental results demonstrate the system's ability to handle critical scenarios by generating comfortable, human-like trajectories. Benchmarks confirm the solver's operational feasibility, achieving convergence in under 50 ms.

</details>


### [349] [GuidedAttention: Interpretable and Correctable Visual Attention for OOD-Robust Robot Manipulation via Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21049)
*Masaki Murooka,Ryoichi Nakajo,Keisuke Shirai,Tomohiro Motoda,Hanbit Oh,Ryo Hanai,Yukiyasu Domae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GuidedAttention框架，通过可解释、可纠正的视觉注意力关键点作为显式中间表征，提升机器人操作策略在分布外条件下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端视觉运动策略缺乏可解释性，人类难以理解或纠正策略的视觉注意力，导致在分布外条件下性能下降。

Method: 从相机图像预测任务相关的注意力关键点，作为显式中间表征来条件化扩散动作策略；用户可在 rollout 初始化时检查并修正关键点，修正后的注意力通过跟踪模块在后续执行中自动传播。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，GuidedAttention 持续提升了机器人操作性能，尤其在位置和外观分布外条件下效果显著。

Conclusion: 引入可解释、可纠正的视觉注意力中间表征是提升模仿学习策略泛化能力和人机协作有效性的可行方向。

Abstract: End-to-end visuomotor policies provide little opportunity for humans to understand or correct the policy's visual attention. We propose GuidedAttention, a visuomotor imitation learning framework that introduces interpretable and correctable visual attention as an explicit intermediate representation. Task-relevant attention keypoints are predicted from camera images and condition a diffusion-based action policy. Users can inspect and optionally correct selected keypoints once at rollout initialization, after which the corrected attention is automatically propagated throughout execution by a tracking module. Experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate that GuidedAttention consistently improves robot manipulation performance, particularly under positional and appearance out-of-distribution (OOD) conditions.

</details>


### [350] [Human-Inspired Framework for Robotic Craniotomy: Integrating Multimodal Fusion and Adaptive Trajectory Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21058)
*Renzhen Le,Xiao Zhang,Di Wu,Yuanyu Wei,Jiachen Zhu,Zhenzhi Ying,Pengfei Zhang,Liming Shu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种仿人闭环机器人开颅框架，通过自适应双轮廓融合算法生成轨迹，结合多模态感知网络（CMA-TCN-Transformer + 自适应贝叶斯滤波器）实现突破检测，并采用原位投影策略动态补偿深度偏差，在牛肋和离体颅骨实验中实现了97%的突破预测准确率、0.048秒检测延迟、0.29mm最大超调量，且未发生硬脑膜损伤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动开颅手术高风险、依赖医生技能，易导致疲劳和硬脑膜损伤；现有机器人开颅系统为开环系统，仅依赖术前影像，无法补偿术中配准误差和组织变形。

Method: 提出仿人闭环控制框架：(1) 自适应双轮廓融合算法生成贴合颅骨曲面的轨迹，保持工具-骨骼相对位姿一致；(2) 多模态两阶段跨模态注意力块（CMA）-时间卷积网络（TCN）-Transformer网络融合力与声学信号，结合自适应贝叶斯滤波器实现鲁棒的突破检测；(3) 原位投影轨迹调整策略在检测到突破后动态补偿深度偏差，安全隔离残余骨质。

Result: 牛肋实验中突破预测准确率97%，检测延迟0.048±0.097s，最大超调量0.29mm；四例离体颅骨实验全部成功完成，无硬脑膜损伤。

Conclusion: 所提出的控制论框架能够实现安全、自主的开颅手术，具备高效的闭环控制能力。

Abstract: Manual craniotomy is a high-risk, skill-dependent procedure associated with surgeon fatigue and potential dural injury. While robotic approaches have improved safety, existing open-loop systems rely solely on preoperative images and cannot compensate for intraoperative registration errors or tissue deformation. To address this, we propose a human-inspired closed-loop robotic craniotomy framework that intelligently integrates preoperative planning with intraoperative execution. An adaptive dual-contour fusion algorithm is employed to generate trajectories that conform to complex cranial geometries while maintaining a consistent tool-bone relative pose. For intraoperative perception, a multimodal two-stage cross-modal attention block (CMA)-temporal convolutional network (TCN)-Transformer network combined with an adaptive Bayesian filter fuses force and acoustic signals to achieve robust breakthrough detection under varying bone conditions. Upon detection, an in-situ projection-based trajectory adjustment strategy dynamically compensates for depth deviations, enabling safe residual bone isolation. Experiments on bovine ribs show a breakthrough prediction accuracy of 97%, a detection latency of 0.048 +/- 0.097 s, and a maximum overshoot of 0.29 mm. All four ex vivo cranial experiments were successfully completed without dural injury. These results demonstrate that the proposed cybernetic framework enables safe and autonomous craniotomy with highly effective closed-loop control.

</details>


### [351] [RL-MACRO: A Cybernetic Closed-Loop Intelligence Framework for Multimodal Adaptive Robotic Craniotomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21113)
*Xiao Zhang,Jiaxuan Li,Renzhen Le,Di Wu,Chao Sun,Jiachen Zhu,Haoyuan Zhang,Xiang Li,Jian Liu,Zhenzhi Ying,Pengfei Zhang,Liming Shu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RL-MACRO，一种面向自主机器人开颅手术的闭环智能框架，通过CNN-LSTM观测器融合力和声音反馈重建隐藏温度状态，并利用离线IQL策略动态优化进给速度、主轴转速和切削深度，在保证安全边界的前提下提升手术效率。实验验证了其在离体骨组织上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人开颅手术中，由于组织特性时变且无法直接测量切削温度（物理遮挡导致部分可观测），传统的开环控制难以同时避免机械过载/热损伤并维持手术效率，亟需一种能从不完全传感反馈中感知隐藏状态并自适应决策的闭环智能框架。

Method: 提出RL-MACRO框架，包含三层：1) CNN-LSTM观测器融合力和声音反馈重构温度状态；2) 以重构温度与多传感器特征构成信念状态，输入离线IQL策略；3) 双头Actor动态协调进给速度、主轴转速和切削深度，通过在线轨迹重规划和速度伺服转化为空间运动。

Result: 温度重建R²=0.939，MAE=1.717°C；在牛肋骨和六只离体山羊颅骨上验证了鲁棒的感知能力、对力/温度超限的自适应恢复能力以及在不规则表面上的平滑执行能力。

Conclusion: RL-MACRO建立了一种数据驱动的控制论范式，实现了安全且高效的自主骨切削，为机器人在部分可观测条件下的手术操作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic craniotomy requires continuous regulation of tool-tissue interactions to mitigate mechanical overload and thermal damage while maintaining surgical efficiency. However, this process is inherently partially observable due to unknown, time-varying tissue properties and the inability to directly measure cutting temperatures under physical occlusion. To address these challenges, we propose RL-MACRO, a cybernetic closed-loop intelligence framework that couples multimodal perception, adaptive decision-making, and robotic execution. This framework empowers the surgical robot to autonomously perceive inaccessible states from partial sensory feedback and dynamically optimize its behaviors under uncertain environment. A CNN-LSTM observer first fuses force and sound feedback to reconstruct the hidden temperature state (R^2=0.939, MAE = 1.717 deg C). This reconstructed temperature, alongside multi-sensor features, forms the belief state for an offline Implicit Q-Learning (IQL) policy. A novel dual-head Actor dynamically coordinates the feed rate, spindle speed, and cutting depth to optimize efficiency within strict safety bounds. These decisions are seamlessly translated into spatial motions via online trajectory re-planning and velocity servoing. Experiments on bovine ribs and six ex vivo goat skulls validate the system's robust perception, adaptive recovery from force/temperature excursions, and smooth execution on irregular surfaces, establishing a data-driven cybernetic paradigm for safe and efficient autonomous bone cutting.

</details>


### [352] [FORGE-plus: Force-Budgeted Recovery for Contact-Rich Assembly with a Frozen LLM Supervisor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21227)
*Kyupaeck Jeff Rah,Midum Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种两层框架，使用冻结的纯文本大语言模型(LLM)为每个物体分配力上限并选择恢复动作，底层控制器强制执行力上限，实现了在脆弱瓶子放置和0.4mm间隙齿轮插入中256/256无破损、平均峰值力5.4N的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力条件强化学习在紧间隙装配中需要确定每个物体的适当力上限，并在不超限的情况下从插入失败中恢复，现有方法缺乏灵活分配力上限和故障恢复的能力。

Method: 两层框架：上层使用冻结的纯文本LLM根据物体分配力上限并基于力签名选择恢复动作；下层控制器强制执行力上限，恢复策略不可增加力上限，隐藏的破坏力阈值仅评估者可知。

Result: 单一策略在脆弱和坚固物体上256/256评估回合无破损；平均峰值力5.4N；注入滑移时力签名恢复策略在两种夹爪上解决40%和64%故障；PPO在严格力约束下失败，学习式释放策略也未成功。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效解决力约束下的紧间隙装配问题，但PPO和学习式释放策略的失败表明严格的力约束对标准RL方法构成挑战，且实验仅在刚体仿真中未做sim-to-real迁移。

Abstract: Force-conditioned reinforcement learning (RL) enables tight-clearance assembly under a commanded force ceiling, but practical deployment requires determining an appropriate force limit for each object and recovering from insertion failures without exceeding it. We present a two-layer framework in which a frozen, text-only large language model (LLM) assigns a per-object force ceiling before execution and selects recovery maneuvers from a fixed action menu using compact textual force signatures. The LLM never controls force directly: a low-level controller enforces the force ceiling, the recovery policy cannot increase it, and the hidden breaking-force threshold is known only to the evaluator.
  We evaluate the framework on fragile bottle placement and 0.4 mm diametral-clearance gear insertion using two grippers (Robotiq 2F-140 and Franka Panda hand). A single policy passes 256/256 evaluation episodes on both fragile and robust objects without breakage, correctly predicts release timing, and completes a full table-pick-and-insert pipeline with a mean peak force of 5.4 N. Under injected in-grip slip, the force-signature recovery strategy resolves 40% and 64% of failures on the two grippers, whereas a press-harder baseline is either ineffective or causes frequent breakage. We also report negative results, including the failure of PPO to solve the task under strict force constraints and unsuccessful learned release strategies. All experiments are conducted in rigid-body simulation with hidden force-threshold breakage; no sim-to-real claim is made.

</details>


### [353] [Factorized Spatio-Temporal Convolutions for Human Pose Estimation from Planar Lidar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21309)
*Simone Arreghini,Mirko Nava,Nicholas Carlotti,Antonio Paolillo,Alessandro Giusti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于时空模块的轻量级网络，利用平面LiDAR序列实现360°人体检测和相对2D姿态估计，通过跨模态自监督训练减少人工标注需求，在服务机器人CPU上实现实时推理，距离、位置和朝向误差分别降低38%、28%和15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人通常仅配备全向平面LiDAR和低算力处理器，现有姿态估计方法依赖摄像头、3D LiDAR或GPU计算，无法满足在计算受限平台上实现实时人体检测和朝向估计的需求。

Method: 提出基于时空模块（Space-Time Blocks）的轻量级网络，沿扫描射线进行空间处理，跨扫描帧进行时间聚合；利用RGB-D人体追踪器在传感器重叠区域提供跨模态自监督信号，无需人工LiDAR标注。

Result: 相比参数匹配的基线模型，距离误差降低38%，位置误差降低28%，朝向误差降低15%；在FROG公开数据集上验证性能；在服务机器人CPU上实现实时推理并通过实地演示验证。

Conclusion: 该方案在计算受限的服务机器人平台上实现了有效的全向人体检测与朝向估计，验证了其适用于空间感知和社交感知的人机交互场景。

Abstract: Localizing nearby humans and estimating their facing direction are key capabilities for safe navigation and socially aware human-robot interaction. Many pose-estimation pipelines target cameras and 3D LiDAR or assume GPU-class compute, whereas service robots are often equipped only with omnidirectional planar LiDARs and modest onboard processors. We address omnidirectional human detection and relative 2D pose estimation from planar LiDAR sequences with a lightweight network based on Space-Time Blocks, which explicitly separate spatial processing along scan rays from temporal aggregation across scans. Our network processes 360° LiDAR sequences to output per-ray human presence, distance, and relative orientation. We train it via cross-modal self-supervision from a narrow RGB-D body tracker in the sensors' overlap region, removing the need for manual LiDAR labels. Quantitative experiments show that our approach consistently outperforms a parameter-matched baseline model, reducing errors in distance (-38%), position (-28%), and orientation (-15%). We further benchmark on the public FROG dataset, report real-time CPU inference on a service robot, and validate with in-field demonstrations, supporting its suitability for spatial perception on computationally constrained service robots.

</details>


### [354] [Grasp, Handover, Rotate: Bimanual Object Reorientation via Compositional Diffusion and Energy-Based Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21341)
*Wun Lam Yeung,Wenjun Liu,Yui Cheung Yu,Zhengyan Lambo Qin,Qijin She,Heng Li,Ziqi Wang,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BiCompoDiff 是一个基于组合扩散和能量模型的框架，用于解决双臂物体重定向问题。它通过将预训练的抓取扩散模型与双臂规划能量模型相结合，在反向扩散过程中注入梯度引导，以同时优化抓取选择、交接、重抓取和运动规划，满足碰撞避免、轨迹平滑、交接可行性和重抓取安全等多重约束。实验表明，该方法在成功率和轨迹平滑度上显著优于基线方法，并实现了有效的仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂物体重定向（拿起物体、在两臂间传递并放置到目标位姿）在直接放置因碰撞、运动学约束或最终姿态不佳而不可行时具有重要价值，但如何在多重竞争目标下实现这一过程仍具有挑战性。

Method: 提出 BiCompoDiff 框架，结合预训练的抓取扩散模型与双臂规划能量模型（EBMs），在反向扩散过程中通过梯度引导注入碰撞避免、轨迹平滑（通过可微逆运动学）、交接可行性和重抓取安全等约束，并采用退火MCMC采样在组合能量景观上优化抓取位姿。

Result: 在多种模拟家庭环境重定向任务中，BiCompoDiff 相比基于采样的强基线方法实现了超过20%的成功率提升和高达37%的轨迹平滑度提升（以关节位移衡量）。真实世界实验验证了有效的仿真到现实迁移及在挑战性场景中的鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: BiCompoDiff 通过组合扩散模型和能量模型的框架，有效解决了双臂重定向中的多重约束联合优化问题，在成功率和轨迹质量方面均显著优于传统方法，且具备良好的真实世界部署能力。

Abstract: Bimanual object reorientation - picking an object, handing it over between two arms, and placing it in a desired target pose - is valuable when direct placement from the initial grasp is infeasible due to collisions, kinematic constraints, or poor final orientation. However, achieving this under multiple competing objectives remains challenging. We introduce BiCompoDiff, a compositional diffusion and energy-based framework that jointly optimizes grasp selection, handover, regrasp, and motion planning under multiple constraints. By combining a pretrained grasp diffusion model with bimanual planning energy-based models (EBMs), our method injects gradient guidance during reverse diffusion to enforce collision avoidance, trajectory smoothness (via differentiable inverse kinematics), handover feasibility, and regrasp safety. Annealed MCMC sampling further refines grasp poses over the composite energy landscape. Experiments across diverse simulated household reorientation tasks demonstrate that BiCompoDiff achieves over 20% higher success rates and up to 37% smoother trajectories (measured by joint displacement) compared to strong sampling-based baselines. Real-world validation confirms effective sim-to-real transfer and robust performance on challenging scenes.

</details>


### [355] [VoLN: Vision-Only Long-Horizon Navigation---Paradigm, Benchmark, and Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21400)
*Jiabin Lou,Haopeng Wang,Yuanshuai Wang,Xinyu Liu,Xuxin Lv,Yuxin Guo,Lei Huang,Rongye Shi,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Vision-Only Long-Horizon Navigation (VoLN)，将路径相关信息从外部指令转移到本地可观测的场景线索中，并构建了无人机导航基准VoLN-UAV（7,210个episode）及基线方法VoLN-MLLM，实验表明在长程导航中仍面临巨大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLN任务中，路径级指令编码了方向、距离、布局等空间先验信息，这些信息在部署时并非来自机载感知，导致评估结果混杂了视觉导航能力和任务描述中显式提供的路径结构信息。作者希望提出一种更纯粹的视觉导航公式，去除外部指令中的路径信息。

Method: 提出VoLN公式，目标视图指定目的地，路径相关信息仅通过本地可观测的场景线索获得。构建VoLN-UAV基准（7,210个episode），结合长程目标导向飞行、连续3D运动、大视角变化和上下文相关的信标选择。提出VoLN-MLLM基线，对齐自监督视觉特征与结构化语义空间，从观测历史、目标视图、检索的视觉-语义token和本体感知预测短程航点段。

Result: 在五个环境的Test-Unseen分割上，VoLN-MLLM在Easy、Normal、Hard情节上分别获得7.4%、4.5%和1.8%的成功率。

Conclusion: 结果为VoLN提供了初步评估，揭示了长程证据整合、跨视角目标匹配和闭环稳定性方面仍存在重大挑战。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) enables embodied agents to follow natural-language instructions. However, route-level instructions commonly encode spatial priors, such as orientation, distance, and layout, that are not explicitly available from onboard sensing at deployment in open, GPS-denied environments. Benchmark performance under such interfaces therefore jointly reflects visual navigation ability and the use of route structure explicitly supplied by the task description. As a complementary formulation, we propose Vision-Only Long-Horizon Navigation (VoLN), which shifts route-relevant information from externally supplied instructions and global guidance to locally observable in-scene cues. In VoLN, goal views specify the destination, while route-relevant information is available only through locally observable in-scene cues that the agent must detect, interpret, and select online.
